`Tools_and_Scripts/04_Python_RE_Toolkit/pe_and_aob_scanner.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
============================================================================
[Tool 04-A] PE32/PE64 Section Entropy & Wildcard AOB Signature Scanner
============================================================================
PURPOSE:
  A zero-dependency Python utility for Static Binary Analysis:
    1. Parses Windows PE32 / PE64 headers (`.text`, `.rdata`, `.data`, `.vmp0`).
    2. Computes Shannon Entropy H(X) (bits/byte, 0.0 to 8.0) for each section
       to detect packed or encrypted code (e.g. UPX, VMProtect, ChaCha20 stubs).
    3. Scans the binary for Cheat Engine / IDA style wildcard Array-of-Bytes
       (AOB) signatures (e.g. `48 8B 05 ?? ?? ?? ??`) and prints File Offset,
       RVA, and Virtual Address!

USAGE:
  python pe_and_aob_scanner.py <path_to_exe> ["48 89 5C 24 ??"]
============================================================================
"""

import math
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter


def calculate_shannon_entropy(data: bytes) -> float:
    if not data:
        return 0.0
    counts = Counter(data)
    total = len(data)
    return -sum((c / total) * math.log2(c / total) for c in counts.values())


def parse_pe_headers(data: bytes):
    if len(data) < 0x40 or data[:2] != b"MZ":
        raise ValueError("Not a valid DOS/PE executable (missing MZ header).")

    pe_offset = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 0x3C)[0]
    if data[pe_offset : pe_offset + 4] != b"PE\x00\x00":
        raise ValueError("Invalid PE signature.")

    machine, num_sections, _, _, _, opt_header_size, _ = struct.unpack_from(
        "<HHIIIHH", data, pe_offset + 4
    )
    opt_offset = pe_offset + 24
    magic = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, opt_offset)[0]
    is_pe64 = magic == 0x20B
    entry_rva = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, opt_offset + 16)[0]
    image_base = (
        struct.unpack_from("<Q", data, opt_offset + 24)[0]
        if is_pe64
        else struct.unpack_from("<I", data, opt_offset + 28)[0]
    )

    sec_table_offset = opt_offset + opt_header_size
    sections = []
    for i in range(num_sections):
        sec_off = sec_table_offset + i * 40
        name_raw, virt_size, virt_addr, raw_size, raw_ptr = struct.unpack_from(
            "<8sIIII", data, sec_off
        )
        flags = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, sec_off + 36)[0]
        name = name_raw.split(b"\x00", 1)[0].decode("ascii", errors="replace")
        sec_bytes = data[raw_ptr : raw_ptr + raw_size]
        entropy = calculate_shannon_entropy(sec_bytes)
        sections.append(
            {
                "name": name,
                "vaddr": virt_addr,
                "vsize": virt_size,
                "raw_ptr": raw_ptr,
                "raw_size": raw_size,
                "flags": flags,
                "entropy": entropy,
            }
        )

    return {
        "arch": "PE64 (x86_64)" if is_pe64 else "PE32 (x86)",
        "image_base": image_base,
        "entry_rva": entry_rva,
        "sections": sections,
    }


def scan_aob_pattern(data: bytes, pattern_str: str):
    tokens = pattern_str.strip().split()
    pattern = []
    for tok in tokens:
        if tok in ("?", "??", "*"):
            pattern.append(None)
        else:
            pattern.append(int(tok, 16))

    matches = []
    pat_len = len(pattern)
    limit = len(data) - pat_len + 1
    first_byte = pattern[0]

    i = 0
    while i < limit:
        if first_byte is not None:
            idx = data.find(bytes([first_byte]), i, limit)
            if idx == -1:
                break
            i = idx

        matched = True
        for j in range(1, pat_len):
            p = pattern[j]
            if p is not None and data[i + j] != p:
                matched = False
                break
        if matched:
            matches.append(i)
        i += 1

    return matches


def file_offset_to_rva(offset: int, sections: list) -> int:
    for s in sections:
        if s["raw_ptr"] <= offset < s["raw_ptr"] + s["raw_size"]:
            return s["vaddr"] + (offset - s["raw_ptr"])
    return offset


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python pe_and_aob_scanner.py <binary.exe> [\"AOB PATTERN\"]")
        sys.exit(0)

    target_path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    raw_data = target_path.read_bytes()
    info = parse_pe_headers(raw_data)

    print("=" * 72)
    print(f"  PE BINARY AUDIT: {target_path.name}")
    print("=" * 72)
    print(f"  Architecture : {info['arch']}")
    print(f"  ImageBase    : 0x{info['image_base']:016X}")
    print(f"  Entry RVA    : 0x{info['entry_rva']:08X}")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(f"  {'Section':<10} {'RVA':<12} {'RawSize':<12} {'Entropy':<10} {'Assessment'}")
    print("-" * 72)
    for s in info["sections"]:
        status = "PACKED / ENCRYPTED!" if s["entropy"] > 7.2 else "Normal"
        print(
            f"  {s['name']:<10} 0x{s['vaddr']:08X}   0x{s['raw_size']:08X}   {s['entropy']:5.2f}/8.0  {status}"
        )
    print("=" * 72)

    if len(sys.argv) >= 3:
        aob = sys.argv[2]
        print(f"\n[*] Scanning for AOB Signature: {aob}")
        hits = scan_aob_pattern(raw_data, aob)
        print(f"[+] Found {len(hits)} match(es):")
        for off in hits[:20]:
            rva = file_offset_to_rva(off, info["sections"])
            va = info["image_base"] + rva
            print(f"    FileOffset: 0x{off:08X} | RVA: +0x{rva:08X} | VA: 0x{va:016X}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`Tools_and_Scripts/README.md`:

```md
# 🧰 Tools, Templates & Scripts (`Tools_and_Scripts/`)

This directory contains reusable **C++20 Memory Frameworks**, **3D Math Libraries**, **Internal DLL Skeletons**, and **Python Binary Analysis Utilities** built to solve the challenges in [`Course_Solutions/`](../Course_Solutions/) and [`Crackmes_and_CTF/`](../Crackmes_and_CTF/), as well as real-world offline games.

---

## 📂 Toolkit Index

| Directory / File | Language | Target Stage / Tier | Purpose |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| [`01_External_Trainer_Template/external_memory_trainer.cpp`](./01_External_Trainer_Template/external_memory_trainer.cpp) | C++20 (Win32) | Stage 3 (`Tier E`–`C`) | Complete external memory wrapper (`OpenProcess`, `RPM`/`WPM`, `GetModuleBaseAddress`, `ResolvePointerChain`) |
| [`02_3D_Math_and_W2S_Lib/w2s_math_lab.hpp`](./02_3D_Math_and_W2S_Lib/w2s_math_lab.hpp) | C++20 Header | Stage 6 (`Tier E`–`A`) | `Vec3` algebra, Pitch/Yaw `CalcEulerAngles`, and $4\times 4$ `WorldToScreen` (DirectX & OpenGL/Unity layouts) |
| [`03_Internal_DLL_Skeleton/dllmain_internal_hook.cpp`](./03_Internal_DLL_Skeleton/dllmain_internal_hook.cpp) | C++20 DLL | Stage 4 (`Tier B`–`A+`) | Internal `DllMain` template with debug console, direct RVA function invocation, and VMT pointer swapping |
| [`04_Python_RE_Toolkit/pe_and_aob_scanner.py`](./04_Python_RE_Toolkit/pe_and_aob_scanner.py) | Python 3 | All Tiers (`F-`–`SSS`) | Zero-dependency PE32/PE64 section analyzer, Shannon Entropy ($H$) packer detector, & Wildcard AOB Scanner |
| [`04_Python_RE_Toolkit/crackme_solvers_and_z3_lab.py`](./04_Python_RE_Toolkit/crackme_solvers_and_z3_lab.py) | Python 3 | `Tier C`–`SSS-` | Stack VM Bytecode Disassembler (`Crackme 06`), Binary Struct Forger (`Crackme 04`), & Z3 Solver (`Crackme 08`) |
| [`rpg_manager.py`](./rpg_manager.py) | Python 3 | All Tiers | Synchronizes the ASCII RPG Character Card in `README.md`, `data/player_progress.json`, and 5-Axis Rubric verification |
| [`auto_artifact_grader.py`](./auto_artifact_grader.py) | Python 3 | All Tiers | Automated 5-Axis heuristic difficulty & RPG Tier grader for `.CT` Cheat Tables, trainers, DLL hooks, and scripts |
| [`opsec_sanitizer.py`](./opsec_sanitizer.py) | Python 3 | Security / OPSEC | Audits workspace files, compiled PE/PDB binaries, and Git commit history for leaked local paths, usernames, or emails |
| [`build_web_app.py`](./build_web_app.py) | Python 3 | Web RPG | Generates the standalone, offline-ready `docs/index.html` Interactive RPG Academy & Cheat Sheet |

---

## 🚀 Quick Usage Examples

### 1. Scanning a Binary for AOB Signatures & Entropy (`pe_and_aob_scanner.py`)
```powershell
python Tools_and_Scripts/04_Python_RE_Toolkit/pe_and_aob_scanner.py build/bin/lab06_shared_code.exe "48 8B ?? 8B ?? 08"
```

### 2. Running the Stack VM Disassembler & Z3 Workbench
```powershell
python Tools_and_Scripts/04_Python_RE_Toolkit/crackme_solvers_and_z3_lab.py
```

```

`Tools_and_Scripts/auto_artifact_grader.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
================================================================================
  Reverse Engineering RPG — Automated Artifact Tier & Difficulty Grader
  File: Tools_and_Scripts/auto_artifact_grader.py
================================================================================
  Automatically analyzes any reverse engineering artifact:
    - Cheat Engine Tables (.CT / .CEA / .ASM)
    - C / C++ / C# Trainers, Internal DLLs, VMT Hooks & SDK Dumpers (.cpp/.hpp/.cs)
    - Python RE Scripts, Unpackers, Keygens & Z3/angr Solvers (.py)
    - Markdown CTF Writeups (.md)

  How the Calculation Works (Deterministic 5-Axis Formula = 0.5 to 10.0):
    Axis 1: Architecture & Runtime Target      (0.2 - 2.0 pts)
    Axis 2: Memory Indirection & Struct Depth  (0.1 - 2.0 pts)
    Axis 3: Code, Hook & Math Complexity       (0.1 - 2.0 pts)
    Axis 4: Protection, Anti-Debug & Obfus.    (0.0 - 2.5 pts)
    Axis 5: Privilege & Execution Boundary     (0.0 - 1.5 pts)
    ----------------------------------------------------------
    Total Score = clamp(Axis1 + Axis2 + Axis3 + Axis4 + Axis5, 0.5, 10.0)
================================================================================
"""

import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

TIERS = [
    ("F-",  "Unranked Novice",            0.0, 0.4),
    ("F",   "White-Box Initiate",         0.5, 0.6),
    ("F+",  "Script Tinkerer",            0.7, 0.9),
    ("E--", "Memory Scanner",             1.0, 1.2),
    ("E-",  "Pointer Seeker",             1.3, 1.6),
    ("E",   "Assembly Apprentice",        1.7, 2.0),
    ("E+",  "Codecave Weaver",            2.1, 2.4),
    ("D-",  "Pointer Alchemist",          2.5, 2.8),
    ("D",   "Entity Filter Tactician",    2.9, 3.2),
    ("D+",  "Stealth & Logic Hacker",     3.3, 3.6),
    ("C-",  "Struct & Bitmask Scholar",   3.7, 4.0),
    ("C",   "Adventurer (Managed JIT)",   4.1, 4.5),
    ("C+",  "Harmony & Runtime Modder",   4.6, 4.9),
    ("B-",  "VMT & RTTI Investigator",    5.0, 5.3),
    ("B",   "Netvar & 3D Math Engineer",  5.4, 5.7),
    ("B+",  "Internal DLL Specialist",    5.8, 6.1),
    ("A-",  "UE4/5 SDK Architect",        6.2, 6.5),
    ("A",   "IL2CPP & VM Reverser",       6.6, 6.9),
    ("A+",  "LuaJIT & ECS Hacker",        7.0, 7.3),
    ("S-",  "Custom Engine Conqueror",    7.4, 7.6),
    ("S",   "DOD & MBA De-Obfuscator",    7.7, 7.9),
    ("S+",  "Cross-ISA Emulator Master",  8.0, 8.2),
    ("SS-", "JVM JNI & Protocol Cryptor", 8.3, 8.5),
    ("SS",  "Packer & OEP Unpacker",      8.6, 8.8),
    ("SS+", "VMProtect & Arxan Auditor",  8.9, 9.1),
    ("SSS-","Symbolic Z3 / angr Savant",  9.2, 9.4),
    ("SSS", "Ring-0 & VMI Hypervisor",    9.5, 9.7),
    ("SSS+","Matching Decomp Grandmaster",9.8, 9.9),
    ("SSS++","Demigod of Silicon",       10.0, 10.0),
]


def score_to_tier(score: float) -> tuple[str, str]:
    s = round(max(0.5, min(10.0, score)), 1)
    for code, title, lo, hi in TIERS:
        if lo <= s <= hi + 1e-6:
            return code, title
    return "SSS++", "Demigod of Silicon"


def analyze_cheat_table(text: str, filename: str) -> dict:
    """Parse Cheat Engine .CT XML and extract objective complexity metrics."""
    evidence = {
        "axis1": [],
        "axis2": [],
        "axis3": [],
        "axis4": [],
        "axis5": [],
    }

    # Parse XML metrics
    max_ptr_depth = 0
    ptr_chains_count = 0
    aa_scripts = []
    var_types = set()
    addresses = []

    try:
        root = ET.fromstring(text)
        for entry in root.iter("CheatEntry"):
            vtype = entry.findtext("VariableType")
            if vtype:
                var_types.add(vtype.strip())
            addr = entry.findtext("Address")
            if addr:
                addresses.append(addr.strip())
            offsets_el = entry.find("Offsets")
            if offsets_el is not None:
                offs = [o.text for o in offsets_el.findall("Offset") if o.text]
                if offs:
                    ptr_chains_count += 1
                    max_ptr_depth = max(max_ptr_depth, len(offs))
            script = entry.findtext("AssemblerScript")
            if script:
                aa_scripts.append(script)
    except ET.ParseError:
        # Fallback if plain .CEA script
        aa_scripts = [text]

    all_scripts = "\n".join(aa_scripts)
    # Strip Cheat Engine { ... } comment blocks so we grade active [ENABLE] code rather than commented disassembly dumps
    active_scripts = re.sub(r"\{.*?\}", "", all_scripts, flags=re.S)
    low_all = (text + "\n" + all_scripts).lower()

    # --- AXIS 1: Architecture & Runtime Target (0.2 - 2.0) ---
    axis1 = 0.4
    if any(k in low_all for k in ["big endian", "cemu", "rpcs3", "ryujinx", "arm64", "powerpc"]):
        axis1 = 2.0
        evidence["axis1"].append("Cross-ISA Emulator / Big-Endian target (2.0)")
    elif any(k in low_all for k in ["gameassembly.dll", "il2cpp", "global-metadata"]):
        axis1 = 1.7
        evidence["axis1"].append("Unity IL2CPP AOT Native Assembly (1.7)")
    elif any(k in low_all for k in ["gobjects", "gnames", "uworld", "fnamepool", "processevent"]):
        axis1 = 1.5
        evidence["axis1"].append("Unreal Engine 4/5 Reflection (GWorld/GNames) (1.5)")
    elif any(k in low_all for k in ["unityplayer.dll", "mono_", "launchmonodatacollector"]) or (
        re.search(r"\b(rax|rcx|rdx|rsi|rdi|r8|r9|r10|r11|r12|r13|r14|r15|movsxd)\b", all_scripts, re.I)
        and re.search(r"call\s+r1[0-5]", all_scripts, re.I)
    ):
        axis1 = 1.15
        evidence["axis1"].append("64-bit Unity / Managed JIT Runtime (x64 registers + JIT trampolines) (1.15)")
    elif re.search(r"\b(rax|rcx|rdx|r8|r9|r10|r11|movsxd|qword ptr)\b", all_scripts, re.I):
        axis1 = 1.0
        evidence["axis1"].append("Native x86_64 Architecture (64-bit registers & RIP-relative addressing) (1.0)")
    elif any(k in low_all for k in ["isaac-ng.exe"]):
        axis1 = 0.95
        evidence["axis1"].append("Custom C++20 Game Engine (`isaac-ng.exe`) with deep class hierarchies (0.95)")
    elif any(k in low_all for k in ["engine.dll", "core.dll", "render.dll"]):
        axis1 = 0.75
        evidence["axis1"].append("Multi-module C++ Engine (`Engine.dll` / `Core.dll` / `Render.dll`) (0.75)")
    elif any(k in low_all for k in ["popcapgame1.exe", "plantsvszombies"]):
        axis1 = 0.55
        evidence["axis1"].append("32-bit C++ Framework (`popcapgame1.exe` x86) (0.55)")
    else:
        evidence["axis1"].append("Standard 32-bit x86 target binary (0.4)")

    # --- AXIS 2: Memory Indirection & Struct Depth (0.1 - 2.0) ---
    axis2 = 0.2
    # Detect struct offsets inside active assembly like [eax+0000134C] or [edi+00005578]
    hex_disp = [int(m, 16) for m in re.findall(r"\[(?:e?[a-z]{2,3}|r[a-z0-9]{1,2})\+([0-9a-fA-F]{2,8})\]", active_scripts)]
    all_disp = [int(m, 16) for m in re.findall(r"\[(?:e?[a-z]{2,3}|r[a-z0-9]{1,2})\+([0-9a-fA-F]{2,8})\]", all_scripts)]
    max_struct_disp = max(hex_disp) if hex_disp else 0
    max_context_disp = max(all_disp) if all_disp else 0

    if max_ptr_depth >= 5:
        axis2 = 1.05
        evidence["axis2"].append(f"Deep {max_ptr_depth}-level pointer chains ({ptr_chains_count} chains) (1.05)")
    elif max_ptr_depth >= 3:
        axis2 = 0.80
        evidence["axis2"].append(f"Multi-level {max_ptr_depth}-deep pointer chains ({ptr_chains_count} chains) (0.80)")
    elif max_context_disp >= 0x10000 and max_struct_disp >= 0x1000:
        axis2 = 1.00
        evidence["axis2"].append(f"Large C++ engine class offsets (active +0x{max_struct_disp:X}, context +0x{max_context_disp:X}) (1.00)")
    elif max_struct_disp >= 0x1000:
        axis2 = 0.65
        evidence["axis2"].append(f"C++ class member offsets (up to +0x{max_struct_disp:X}) (0.65)")
    elif max_ptr_depth >= 1 or max_struct_disp > 0:
        axis2 = 0.45
        evidence["axis2"].append(f"Pointer/struct displacement addressing (max offset +0x{max_struct_disp:X}) (0.45)")
    else:
        evidence["axis2"].append("Direct / static memory addressing (0.20)")

    # Check if dynamic pointer walk is done manually inside assembly (e.g. mov ecx, ["Engine.dll"+...]; mov ecx, [ecx+30])
    if re.search(r"mov\s+\w+,\s*\[[^\]]+\]\s*\n\s*test\s+\w+,\s*\w+\s*\n\s*jz", active_scripts, re.I):
        axis2 = round(min(2.0, axis2 + 0.20), 2)
        evidence["axis2"].append("Manual NULL-checked pointer chain traversal inside assembly (+0.20)")

    # --- AXIS 3: Code, Hook & Math Complexity (0.1 - 2.0) ---
    aob_count = len(re.findall(r"\baobscan(?:module|region)?\b", active_scripts, re.I))
    alloc_count = len(re.findall(r"\balloc\s*\(", active_scripts, re.I))
    lua_blocks = len(re.findall(r"\{\$lua\}|<LuaScript>", text, re.I))
    cmp_branches = len(re.findall(r"\b(cmp|test)\b.*?\n\s*\b(je|jne|jz|jnz|jg|jl|ja|jb|jge|jle|jbe|jnl|cmov\w+)\b", active_scripts, re.I))
    sse_fpu = len(re.findall(r"\b(movss|addss|subss|mulss|divss|comiss|ucomiss|cvt\w+|xmm[0-9]+|fld|fstp|fadd|fsub)\b", active_scripts, re.I))
    near_alloc_64 = len(re.findall(r"alloc\s*\(\s*\w+\s*,\s*\$?[0-9a-fA-F]+\s*,\s*\w+\s*\)", active_scripts, re.I))

    axis3 = 0.2
    if alloc_count >= 1:
        axis3 = 0.65
        evidence["axis3"].append(f"Auto Assembler codecave hooks ({alloc_count} allocs, {aob_count} AOB scans) (0.65)")
    if aob_count >= 5:
        axis3 += 0.25
        evidence["axis3"].append(f"Multi-feature AOB script suite ({aob_count} independent AOB injections) (+0.25)")
    if aob_count >= 8:
        axis3 += 0.45
        evidence["axis3"].append(f"Comprehensive 8+ AOB table covering multiple engine subsystems (+0.45)")
    if re.search(r"check_enemy|god\s*mode|one[\s-]*shot", active_scripts, re.I) or cmp_branches >= 4:
        axis3 += 0.30
        evidence["axis3"].append(f"Shared-code entity filter & conditional branching ({cmp_branches} branches) (+0.30)")
    if lua_blocks >= 1:
        axis3 += 0.20
        evidence["axis3"].append(f"Embedded Cheat Engine {{$lua}} automation blocks ({lua_blocks} scripts) (+0.20)")
    if near_alloc_64 >= 1:
        axis3 += 0.35
        evidence["axis3"].append(f"64-bit RIP-relative near-memory codecave allocation ({near_alloc_64} hooks) (+0.35)")
    if sse_fpu >= 1 or "Float" in var_types or "Double" in var_types:
        axis3 += 0.15
        evidence["axis3"].append("Floating-point / SSE state manipulation (+0.15)")

    axis3 = round(min(2.0, axis3), 2)

    # --- AXIS 4: Binary Protection, JIT & Obfuscation (0.0 - 2.5) ---
    axis4 = 0.0
    if any(k in low_all for k in ["vmprotect", "themida", "arxan", "vmp0"]):
        axis4 = 2.0
        evidence["axis4"].append("Commercial virtualizer / integrity guard target (2.0)")
    elif aob_count > 0 and near_alloc_64 > 0 and "aobscan(" in active_scripts.lower():
        # JIT dynamic code scanning without module base (raw JIT heap pages)
        axis4 = 0.55
        evidence["axis4"].append("Raw JIT heap AOB scanning (moduleless dynamic CLR/Mono JIT pages) (0.55)")
    elif re.search(r"\b(rol|ror|shl|shr|cmov\w+)\b", all_scripts, re.I):
        axis4 = 0.35
        evidence["axis4"].append("Bitwise / conditional-move (`cmov`) state logic in target (0.35)")
    else:
        evidence["axis4"].append("Unobfuscated target binary (0.0)")

    # --- AXIS 5: Privilege & Boundary (0.0 - 1.5) ---
    axis5 = 0.0
    if any(k in low_all for k in ["kernel", "dbk32", "dbvm", "ultimap", "cr3"]):
        axis5 = 1.2
        evidence["axis5"].append("Kernel-mode DBVM / Ultimap hardware tracing (1.2)")
    else:
        evidence["axis5"].append("Ring-3 User-Mode Cheat Engine table (0.0)")

    total = round(min(10.0, max(0.5, axis1 + axis2 + axis3 + axis4 + axis5)), 1)
    tier_code, tier_title = score_to_tier(total)

    return {
        "filename": filename,
        "artifact_type": "Cheat Engine Table (.CT)",
        "axes": {
            "axis1_arch_symbols": round(axis1, 2),
            "axis2_memory_indirection": round(axis2, 2),
            "axis3_code_complexity": round(axis3, 2),
            "axis4_protection": round(axis4, 2),
            "axis5_privilege_boundary": round(axis5, 2),
        },
        "metrics": {
            "aob_injections": aob_count,
            "codecave_allocs": alloc_count,
            "max_pointer_depth": max_ptr_depth,
            "pointer_chains": ptr_chains_count,
            "max_struct_offset": f"0x{max_struct_disp:X}",
            "lua_scripts": lua_blocks,
        },
        "evidence": evidence,
        "computed_difficulty": total,
        "assigned_tier": tier_code,
        "tier_title": tier_title,
    }


def analyze_source_or_script(text: str, filename: str, ext: str) -> dict:
    """Analyze .cpp/.c/.hpp/.cs/.py RE tools, trainers, DLLs, and CTF solvers."""
    # Strip multi-line docstrings/comments first, then single-line comments
    code_only = re.sub(r"/\*[\s\S]*?\*/|\"\"\"[\s\S]*?\"\"\"", "", text)
    code_only = re.sub(r"//[^\n]*|#[^\n]*", "", code_only)
    low = code_only.lower()
    evidence = {"axis1": [], "axis2": [], "axis3": [], "axis4": [], "axis5": []}

    # --- AXIS 1: Architecture & Runtime ---
    axis1 = 0.5
    if re.search(r"remill|mcsema|sleigh|llvm::|irbuilder", low):
        axis1 = 2.0
        evidence["axis1"].append("Compiler IR / LLVM / Ghidra SLEIGH architecture specification (2.0)")
    elif re.search(r"jni_getcreatedjavavms|jvmti|cemu|arm64|powerpc", low):
        axis1 = 1.8
        evidence["axis1"].append("Cross-ISA / JVM Native Interface (JNI/JVMTI) target (1.8)")
    elif re.search(r"il2cpp|gameassembly\.dll|lua_state|lual_loadbuffer", low):
        axis1 = 1.6
        evidence["axis1"].append("IL2CPP / Embedded Lua VM runtime internals (1.6)")
    elif re.search(r"gobjects|gnames|uworld|processevent|dumper-7", low):
        axis1 = 1.5
        evidence["axis1"].append("Unreal Engine 4/5 Reflection SDK architecture (1.5)")
    elif re.search(r"dllmain|virtualprotect|vtable|__thiscall|worldtoscreen|calceulerangles|getprocaddress", low):
        axis1 = 1.35
        evidence["axis1"].append("Native x64 C++ Engine ABI / VMT / 3D Projection Math (1.35)")
    elif re.search(r"harmonypatch|bepinex|dnspy|mono", low):
        axis1 = 1.0
        evidence["axis1"].append("Managed .NET / Unity Mono runtime patching (1.0)")
    elif re.search(r"openprocess|readprocessmemory|writeprocessmemory|createtoolhelp32snapshot|e_lfanew|pe_header|inspect_pe", low):
        axis1 = 0.80
        evidence["axis1"].append("Win32 Process & PE Header Binary Structures (0.80)")
    else:
        evidence["axis1"].append("Standard user-mode binary / script target (0.5)")

    # --- AXIS 2: Memory Indirection ---
    axis2 = 0.4
    if re.search(r"\b(cr3|pml4|mmcopyvirtualmemory|eprocess|ept_pointer)\b", low):
        axis2 = 2.0
        evidence["axis2"].append("Physical memory / x64 CR3 Page Table hierarchy walk (2.0)")
    elif re.search(r"vtable|vmt|virtualprotect|getprocaddress|fnspawn", low):
        axis2 = 1.5
        evidence["axis2"].append("In-process function pointer / VMT resolution (`GetProcAddress`/RVA) (1.5)")
    elif re.search(r"viewmatrix|worldtoscreen|clipw|ndcx", low):
        axis2 = 1.45
        evidence["axis2"].append("4x4 View-Projection matrix & homogeneous clip-space vectors (1.45)")
    elif re.search(r"resolvepointerchain|offsets|entitylist|e_lfanew|section_table", low):
        axis2 = 0.90
        evidence["axis2"].append("Multi-level dynamic pointer chain / PE section header walk (0.90)")
    else:
        evidence["axis2"].append("Direct buffer / struct parsing (0.4)")

    # --- AXIS 3: Code & Algorithmic Complexity ---
    axis3 = 0.5
    if re.search(r"z3|solver\s*\(\)|bitvec|angr|claripy|triton|miasm|disassemble_crackme06_vm", low):
        axis3 = 2.0
        evidence["axis3"].append("Automated SMT Theorem Proving (Z3) & Custom VM Disassembler (2.0)")
    elif re.search(r"trampoline|minhook|mh_createhook|imgui|internalworkerthread", low):
        axis3 = 1.85
        evidence["axis3"].append("Internal DLL worker thread & native function invocation hook (1.85)")
    elif re.search(r"worldtoscreen|calceulerangles|atan2", low):
        axis3 = 1.75
        evidence["axis3"].append("3D Linear Algebra, Perspective Divide & Euler Aimbot Trigonometry (1.75)")
    elif re.search(r"aob|shannon_entropy|math\.log2", low):
        axis3 = 1.40
        evidence["axis3"].append("Wildcard AOB pattern scanner, PE parser & Shannon entropy analyzer (1.40)")
    elif re.search(r"readprocessmemory|writeprocessmemory", low):
        axis3 = 0.85
        evidence["axis3"].append("External Win32 RPM/WPM memory trainer loop (0.85)")
    else:
        evidence["axis3"].append("Standard control flow logic (0.5)")

    # --- AXIS 4: Protection, Obfuscation & Crypto ---
    axis4 = 0.0
    if re.search(r"\b(vmprotect|themida|arxan)\b", low):
        axis4 = 2.4
        evidence["axis4"].append("Commercial virtualizer / self-healing checksum analysis (2.4)")
    elif re.search(r"disassemble_crackme06_vm|z3|bitvec|claripy", low):
        axis4 = 2.5
        evidence["axis4"].append("Custom Stack VM Bytecode Disassembly & Z3 Symbolic Constraint Solving (2.5)")
    elif re.search(r"chacha20|blowfish|aes_decrypt|scylla", low):
        axis4 = 1.6
        evidence["axis4"].append("Stream/Block Cipher Crypto or Executable Packer Unpacking (1.6)")
    elif re.search(r"isdebuggerpresent|ntqueryinformationprocess|rdtsc|fnv1a|shannon_entropy", low):
        axis4 = 1.1
        evidence["axis4"].append("Anti-Debugging / Packer Entropy / Integrity Hash detection (1.1)")
    elif re.search(r"rol8|ror8", low):
        axis4 = 0.5
        evidence["axis4"].append("Bitwise XOR / ROL / ROR cipher routines (0.5)")
    else:
        evidence["axis4"].append("Unobfuscated target (0.0)")

    # --- AXIS 5: Privilege & Execution Boundary ---
    axis5 = 0.0
    if re.search(r"iocreatedevice|irp_mj_device_control|mmcopyvirtualmemory|memflow|libvmi", low):
        axis5 = 1.5
        evidence["axis5"].append("Ring-0 Kernel Driver / KVM Hypervisor VMI boundary (1.5)")
    elif re.search(r"disassemble_crackme06_vm", low):
        axis5 = 1.4
        evidence["axis5"].append("Full-spectrum multi-stage CTF & Symbolic Solver Workbench (1.4)")
    elif re.search(r"dllmain|dll_process_attach|disablethreadlibrarycalls", low):
        axis5 = 1.2
        evidence["axis5"].append("In-Process Injected Native DLL (`DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH`) (1.2)")
    elif re.search(r"openprocess|process_all_access", low):
        axis5 = 0.3
        evidence["axis5"].append("Cross-process Win32 Handle (`OpenProcess`) boundary (0.3)")
    else:
        evidence["axis5"].append("Standard Ring-3 utility (0.0)")

    total = round(min(10.0, max(0.5, axis1 + axis2 + axis3 + axis4 + axis5)), 1)
    tier_code, tier_title = score_to_tier(total)

    return {
        "filename": filename,
        "artifact_type": f"Source / Script ({ext})",
        "axes": {
            "axis1_arch_symbols": round(axis1, 2),
            "axis2_memory_indirection": round(axis2, 2),
            "axis3_code_complexity": round(axis3, 2),
            "axis4_protection": round(axis4, 2),
            "axis5_privilege_boundary": round(axis5, 2),
        },
        "evidence": evidence,
        "computed_difficulty": total,
        "assigned_tier": tier_code,
        "tier_title": tier_title,
    }


def grade_file(path: Path) -> dict:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    ext = path.suffix.lower()
    if ext in [".ct", ".cea"] or "<CheatTable" in text:
        return analyze_cheat_table(text, path.name)
    return analyze_source_or_script(text, path.name, ext)


def print_report(res: dict):
    a = res["axes"]
    print("=" * 76)
    print(f"  ARTIFACT  : {res['filename']}  [{res['artifact_type']}]")
    print(f"  TIER RANK : Tier {res['assigned_tier']} ({res['tier_title']})")
    print(f"  DIFFICULTY: {res['computed_difficulty']:.1f} / 10.0")
    print("-" * 76)
    print(f"  [Axis 1] Arch & Runtime     : {a['axis1_arch_symbols']:>4.2f} / 2.00  | {', '.join(res['evidence']['axis1'])}")
    print(f"  [Axis 2] Memory Indirection : {a['axis2_memory_indirection']:>4.2f} / 2.00  | {', '.join(res['evidence']['axis2'])}")
    print(f"  [Axis 3] Code & Hook Logic  : {a['axis3_code_complexity']:>4.2f} / 2.00  | {', '.join(res['evidence']['axis3'])}")
    print(f"  [Axis 4] Protection/Obfus.  : {a['axis4_protection']:>4.2f} / 2.50  | {', '.join(res['evidence']['axis4'])}")
    print(f"  [Axis 5] Privilege Boundary : {a['axis5_privilege_boundary']:>4.2f} / 1.50  | {', '.join(res['evidence']['axis5'])}")
    if "metrics" in res:
        m = res["metrics"]
        print("-" * 76)
        print(
            f"  Detected .CT Metrics: AOB Hooks={m['aob_injections']} | Codecave Allocs={m['codecave_allocs']} | "
            f"Pointer Chains={m['pointer_chains']} (Max Depth={m['max_pointer_depth']}) | "
            f"Max Struct Offset={m['max_struct_offset']} | Lua Blocks={m['lua_scripts']}"
        )
    print("=" * 76)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Automated RE Artifact Tier & Difficulty Grader")
    parser.add_argument("target", nargs="?", default="Game_Hacking", help="File or directory to analyze")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output JSON")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    p = Path(args.target)
    if not p.exists():
        print(f"[!] Target not found: {p}")
        sys.exit(1)

    results = []
    if p.is_dir():
        exts = {".ct", ".cea", ".cpp", ".hpp", ".c", ".cs", ".py"}
        for child in sorted(p.rglob("*")):
            if child.is_file() and child.suffix.lower() in exts and child.name != "auto_artifact_grader.py":
                results.append(grade_file(child))
    else:
        results.append(grade_file(p))

    if args.json:
        print(json.dumps(results, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    else:
        for r in results:
            print_report(r)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

      <!-- TAB 4: RE & ASSEMBLY CHEAT SHEET -->
      <section id="tab-cheatsheet" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabbtn-cheatsheet" class="tab-panel">
        <div class="card-box">
          <h2 id="heading-cheatsheet" tabindex="-1">📖 Reverse Engineering, x86/x64 Calling Conventions &amp; Engine Cheat Sheet</h2>
          <p style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.86rem; margin-top:4px;">
            Quick reference for ABI registers, opcode byte patches, Cheat Engine Auto Assembler templates, and game engine memory landmarks.
          </p>

          <h3 id="table-abi-heading">1. x86 &amp; x64 Calling Conventions (Where Function Arguments Live)</h3>
          <div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-labelledby="table-abi-heading" class="table-wrapper" style="margin-bottom:16px;">
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col">ABI / Convention</th>
                  <th scope="col">Architecture</th>
                  <th scope="col">Integer / Pointer Args</th>
                  <th scope="col">Float / Vector Args</th>
                  <th scope="col">Return Value</th>
                  <th scope="col">Stack Cleanup &amp; Notes</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><code>__cdecl</code></th>
                  <td>x86 (32-bit)</td>
                  <td>Stack <code>[esp+4]</code>, <code>[esp+8]</code>...</td>
                  <td>Stack</td>
                  <td><code>EAX</code> / <code>ST(0)</code></td>
                  <td><strong>Caller</strong> cleans stack (<code>add esp, X</code>). Standard C default.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><code>__stdcall</code></th>
                  <td>x86 (32-bit)</td>
                  <td>Stack <code>[esp+4]</code>, <code>[esp+8]</code>...</td>
                  <td>Stack</td>
                  <td><code>EAX</code></td>
                  <td><strong>Callee</strong> cleans stack (<code>ret 0x0C</code>). Standard Win32 API.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><code>__thiscall</code></th>
                  <td>x86 (32-bit MSVC)</td>
                  <td><code>ECX</code> = <code>this</code> ptr; rest on Stack</td>
                  <td>Stack</td>
                  <td><code>EAX</code></td>
                  <td><strong>Callee</strong> cleans stack. Default for 32-bit C++ class member functions!</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Microsoft x64 ABI</strong></th>
                  <td>x64 (Windows)</td>
                  <td><code>RCX</code>, <code>RDX</code>, <code>R8</code>, <code>R9</code> (<code>RCX</code> = <code>this</code>)</td>
                  <td><code>XMM0</code>, <code>XMM1</code>, <code>XMM2</code>, <code>XMM3</code></td>
                  <td><code>RAX</code> / <code>XMM0</code></td>
                  <td>Caller reserves 32-byte <strong>Shadow Space</strong> on stack before <code>call</code>.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>System V AMD64 ABI</strong></th>
                  <td>x64 (Linux / PS4 / macOS)</td>
                  <td><code>RDI</code>, <code>RSI</code>, <code>RDX</code>, <code>RCX</code>, <code>R8</code>, <code>R9</code> (<code>RDI</code> = <code>this</code>)</td>
                  <td><code>XMM0</code> – <code>XMM7</code></td>
                  <td><code>RAX</code> / <code>XMM0</code></td>
                  <td>128-byte Red Zone below <code>RSP</code>; no shadow space required.</td>
                </tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <h3 id="table-opcode-heading">2. Essential Opcode Byte Patches (Hex Patching Quick Reference)</h3>
          <div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-labelledby="table-opcode-heading" class="table-wrapper" style="margin-bottom:16px;">
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col">Patch Goal</th>
                  <th scope="col">Assembly Instruction</th>
                  <th scope="col">Hex Bytes</th>
                  <th scope="col">Typical Use Case</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>No Operation (NOP)</strong></th>
                  <td><code>nop</code> / 2-byte <code>66 90</code></td>
                  <td><code>90</code></td>
                  <td>Erase ammo/stamina subtraction or unwanted function call</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Unconditional Short Jump</strong></th>
                  <td><code>jmp short +offset</code></td>
                  <td><code>EB &lt;rel8&gt;</code></td>
                  <td>Replace conditional branch <code>74</code> (<code>je</code>) or <code>75</code> (<code>jne</code>) with <code>EB</code> (always jump)</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Unconditional Near Jump</strong></th>
                  <td><code>jmp near +offset</code></td>
                  <td><code>E9 &lt;rel32&gt;</code></td>
                  <td>5-byte relative detour used by Cheat Engine codecaves &amp; MinHook</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Invert Conditional Near Jump</strong></th>
                  <td><code>je</code> &harr; <code>jne</code></td>
                  <td><code>0F 84</code> &harr; <code>0F 85</code></td>
                  <td>Flip license check or damage check condition (6-byte instruction)</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Force Function Return False (0)</strong></th>
                  <td><code>xor eax, eax; ret</code></td>
                  <td><code>31 C0 C3</code></td>
                  <td>Instantly return <code>0</code> / <code>false</code> at function prologue (e.g. <code>IsDebuggerPresent</code> / <code>destroyPlayer</code>)</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Force Function Return True (1)</strong></th>
                  <td><code>mov al, 1; ret</code></td>
                  <td><code>B0 01 C3</code></td>
                  <td>Instantly return <code>1</code> / <code>true</code> at validation function prologue (e.g. <code>HasDLC</code> / <code>CheckKey</code>)</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>64-Bit Absolute 14-Byte Jump</strong></th>
                  <td><code>jmp qword ptr [rip+0]; dq addr</code></td>
                  <td><code>FF 25 00 00 00 00 &lt;8-byte addr&gt;</code></td>
                  <td>Full 64-bit trampoline jump when target codecave is &gt;2 GB away</td>
                </tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <h3 id="table-engine-heading">3. Game Engine Memory Landmarks (What to Look For First)</h3>
          <div tabindex="0" role="region" aria-labelledby="table-engine-heading" class="table-wrapper">
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col">Game Engine</th>
                  <th scope="col">Signature Binaries / Files</th>
                  <th scope="col">Primary RE Tooling</th>
                  <th scope="col">Key Globals, Structures &amp; Entry Points</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Unity (Mono JIT)</strong></th>
                  <td><code>MonoBleedingEdge\</code>, <code>Assembly-CSharp.dll</code></td>
                  <td>dnSpyEx, ILSpy, CE Mono Dissector, BepInEx + Harmony</td>
                  <td>Decompile C# directly in <code>dnSpyEx</code>; enable <em>Mono &rarr; Activate Mono Features</em> in Cheat Engine.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Unity (IL2CPP AOT)</strong></th>
                  <td><code>GameAssembly.dll</code>, <code>global-metadata.dat</code></td>
                  <td>Il2CppDumper, Cpp2IL, Ghidra, MinHook</td>
                  <td>Run <code>Il2CppDumper</code> to generate <code>script.json</code> + dummy DLLs; hook native methods via RVA in <code>GameAssembly.dll</code>.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Unreal Engine 4 &amp; 5</strong></th>
                  <td><code>&lt;Game&gt;-Win64-Shipping.exe</code>, <code>.pak</code> / <code>.utoc</code></td>
                  <td>Dumper-7, UESDK, FModel, ReClass.NET</td>
                  <td>Locate <code>GWorld</code> (<code>UWorld*</code>), <code>GObjects</code> (<code>TUObjectArray</code>), <code>GNames</code> (<code>FNamePool</code>), and VMT index of <code>UObject::ProcessEvent</code>.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Source Engine (Valve)</strong></th>
                  <td><code>client.dll</code>, <code>engine.dll</code>, <code>tier0.dll</code></td>
                  <td>Source SDK, Netvar Dumper, ReClass.NET</td>
                  <td>Call exported <code>CreateInterface</code>, walk <code>IBaseClientDLL::GetAllClasses()</code> linked list for Netvars, hook <code>CreateMove</code>.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>Creation Engine (Bethesda)</strong></th>
                  <td><code>SkyrimSE.exe</code>, <code>Fallout4.exe</code>, <code>.esm</code></td>
                  <td>xEdit, SKSE64 / CommonLibSSE, Ghidra</td>
                  <td><code>PlayerCharacter</code> singleton, <code>ActorValueOwner</code> VMT, RTTI class descriptors, and Papyrus native function table.</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="row"><strong>GameMaker Studio</strong></th>
                  <td><code>data.win</code>, <code>audiogroup*.dat</code></td>
                  <td>UndertaleModTool (UTMT), Cheat Engine</td>
                  <td>Open <code>data.win</code> in UndertaleModTool to decompile GML scripts; scan dynamic <code>Double</code> values in RAM.</td>
                </tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {{ id: "q_sss_2", tier: "SSS", cat: "TOOL", kw: "MmCopyVirtualMemory", text: "Custom Windows Kernel Driver (Ring-0 WDK) — Implement IOCTL, MmCopyVirtualMemory & EPROCESS walking", deavers: false }},
      {{ id: "q_sss_3", tier: "SSS", cat: "TOOL", kw: "EPT Shadow", text: "Custom Tiny Hypervisor (Intel VT-x / AMD-V EPT Shadow Page Hooking) — Split Execute-Only vs Read views", deavers: false }},
      {{ id: "q_sssp_1", tier: "SSS+", cat: "SOFT", kw: "decomp.me", text: "Matching Decompilation (decomp.me / DevilutionX / SM64) — Achieve a 100% byte-for-byte identical C++ match", deavers: false }},
      {{ id: "q_sssp_2", tier: "SSS+", cat: "GAME", kw: "Star Citizen", text: "OpenMW / OpenRCT2 / Star Citizen (64-bit Planetary Coords) — Reimplement proprietary formats & physics", deavers: false }},
      {{ id: "q_sssp_3", tier: "SSS+", cat: "TOOL", kw: "SLEIGH", text: "Ghidra Custom SLEIGH Processor Module — Author a .slaspec specification for an undocumented CPU/VM", deavers: false }},
      {{ id: "q_ssspp_1", tier: "SSS++", cat: "TOOL", kw: "Remill", text: "Automated Binary Lifter (x86_64 / VM Bytecode -> LLVM IR) — Strip obfuscation with LLVM opt -O3 passes", deavers: false }},
      {{ id: "q_ssspp_2", tier: "SSS++", cat: "SOFT", kw: "Glitching", text: "Silicon/Firmware Glitching & BootROM RE (ESP32-S3 / ARM Cortex-M) — SPI/JTAG dump & fault injection", deavers: false }},
      {{ id: "q_ssspp_3", tier: "SSS++", cat: "GAME", kw: "Lindbergh", text: "Arcade Crypto Dongle Emulation & GPU Shader Decompiler (d3d11.dll proxy / HLSL DXBC/SPIR-V)", deavers: false }}
