    const STORAGE_KEY = "re_rpg_journey_state_v2";
    let state = {{
      playerName: "New Challenger",
      theme: "dimmed",
      brightness: 95,
      checked: {{}},
      customQuests: []
    }};

    let lastGradedResult = null;

    function changeTheme(themeName) {{
      document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeName);
      state.theme = themeName;
      saveState();
    }}

    function changeBrightness(val) {{
      document.getElementById("appRoot").style.filter = `brightness(${{val}}%)`;
      state.brightness = parseInt(val, 10);
      try {{ localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state)); }} catch(e) {{}}
    }}

    function loadState() {{
      try {{
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || localStorage.getItem("re_rpg_journey_state_v1");
        if (raw) state = Object.assign(state, JSON.parse(raw));
      }} catch (e) {{}}
      document.getElementById("uiPlayerName").value = state.playerName || "New Challenger";
      document.getElementById("themeSelect").value = state.theme || "dimmed";
      document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", state.theme || "dimmed");
      const br = state.brightness || 95;
      document.getElementById("brightnessRange").value = br;
      document.getElementById("appRoot").style.filter = `brightness(${{br}}%)`;
    }}

    function saveState() {{
      state.playerName = document.getElementById("uiPlayerName").value.trim() || "New Challenger";
      try {{
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
        const pill = document.getElementById("saveStatusPill");
        pill.textContent = "💾 Saved in Browser (" + new Date().toLocaleTimeString() + ")";
      }} catch (e) {{}}
      recalcAndRender();
    }}

    document.getElementById("uiPlayerName").addEventListener("input", saveState);

    function getAllQuests() {{
      const top200Quests = TOP200_GAMES.map(g => ({{
        id: "top200_" + g.rank,
        tier: g.tier,
        cat: "GAME",
        kw: g.title.split(" ")[0],
        text: `[Top #${{g.rank}}] ${{g.title}} (${{g.engine}}) — ${{g.what_to_find}}`,
        isTop200: true
      }}));
      return DEFAULT_QUESTS.concat(state.customQuests || []).concat(top200Quests);
    }}

    function scoreToTierObj(score) {{
      const s = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(10.0, Math.round(score * 10) / 10));
      for (const t of TIER_META) {{
        if (s >= t.lo - 0.01 && s <= t.hi + 0.01) return t;
      }}
      return TIER_META[TIER_META.length - 1];
    }}

    function updateRubricPreview() {{
      const a1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("axArch").value);
      const a2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("axMem").value);
      const a3 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("axEng").value);
      const a4 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("axProt").value);
      const a5 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("axPriv").value);
      const total = Math.min(10.0, Math.max(0.5, +(a1 + a2 + a3 + a4 + a5).toFixed(1)));
      const tObj = scoreToTierObj(total);
      document.getElementById("rubricPreview").textContent =
        `Computed Score: ${{total.toFixed(1)}} / 10.0  ➔  Assigned Tier: [Tier ${{tObj.code}} — ${{tObj.title}}]`;
      return {{ total, tier: tObj.code, rubric: {{ arch_symbols: a1, memory_indirection: a2, engine_complexity: a3, binary_protection: a4, privilege_boundary: a5 }} }};
    }}

    function addCustomTargetToLog() {{
      const title = document.getElementById("addTitle").value.trim() || "Custom RE Target";
      const cat = document.getElementById("addCat").value;
      const quest = document.getElementById("addQuest").value.trim() || "Reverse structures, memory pointers, and control flow";
      const {{ total, tier, rubric }} = updateRubricPreview();

      const newQ = {{
        id: "cust_" + Date.now(),
        tier: tier,
        cat: cat,
        kw: title,
        text: `[Custom ${{total.toFixed(1)}}/10] ${{title}} — ${{quest}}`
      }};
      state.customQuests.push(newQ);
      state.checked[newQ.id] = true;
      saveState();

      const jsonSnippet = {{
        id: newQ.id,
        title: title,
        category: cat === "GAME" ? "Game" : "Software",
        computed_tier: tier,
        computed_score: total,
        rubric: rubric,
        quest: quest,
        completed: true
      }};
      document.getElementById("jsonOutput").textContent = JSON.stringify(jsonSnippet, null, 2);
    }}

    /* =========================================================================
       AUTOMATED ARTIFACT GRADER (.CT / .CEA / .CPP / .CS / .PY)
       ========================================================================= */
    function gradeArtifactText(text, filename) {{
      const lowerName = (filename || "").toLowerCase();
      const isCT = lowerName.endsWith(".ct") || lowerName.endsWith(".cea") || text.includes("<CheatTable") || text.includes("[ENABLE]");
      const ev = {{ a1: [], a2: [], a3: [], a4: [], a5: [] }};
      let a1 = 0.4, a2 = 0.2, a3 = 0.2, a4 = 0.0, a5 = 0.0;
      let metricsText = "";

      if (isCT) {{
        const activeText = text.replace(/\\{{[\\s\\S]*?\\}}/g, "");
        const lowAll = text.toLowerCase();

        const offsetBlocks = text.match(/<Offsets>[\\s\\S]*?<\\/Offsets>/gi) || [];
        let maxPtrDepth = 0;
        offsetBlocks.forEach(blk => {{
          const offs = blk.match(/<Offset>/gi) || [];
          if (offs.length > maxPtrDepth) maxPtrDepth = offs.length;
        }});

        const dispMatches = [...activeText.matchAll(/\\[(?:e?[a-z]{{2,3}}|r[a-z0-9]{{1,2}})\\+([0-9a-fA-F]{{2,8}})\\]/g)];
        const allDispMatches = [...text.matchAll(/\\[(?:e?[a-z]{{2,3}}|r[a-z0-9]{{1,2}})\\+([0-9a-fA-F]{{2,8}})\\]/g)];
        const maxDisp = dispMatches.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, parseInt(x[1], 16)), 0);
        const maxContextDisp = allDispMatches.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, parseInt(x[1], 16)), 0);

        const aobCount = (activeText.match(/\\baobscan(?:module|region)?\\b/gi) || []).length;
        const allocCount = (activeText.match(/\\balloc\\s*\\(/gi) || []).length;
        const luaCount = (text.match(/\\{{\\$lua\\}}|<LuaScript>/gi) || []).length;
        const nearAlloc64 = (activeText.match(/alloc\\s*\\(\\s*\\w+\\s*,\\s*\\$?[0-9a-fA-F]+\\s*,\\s*\\w+\\s*\\)/gi) || []).length;
        const cmpBranches = (activeText.match(/\\b(cmp|test)\\b[\\s\\S]*?\\b(je|jne|jz|jnz|jg|jl|ja|jb|jle|jge|jbe|jnl)\\b/gi) || []).length;

        if (/big endian|cemu|rpcs3|ryujinx|arm64|powerpc/i.test(lowAll)) {{
          a1 = 2.0; ev.a1.push("Cross-ISA Emulator / Big-Endian target (2.0)");
        }} else if (/gameassembly\\.dll|il2cpp|global-metadata/i.test(lowAll)) {{
          a1 = 1.7; ev.a1.push("Unity IL2CPP Native Assembly (1.7)");
        }} else if (/gobjects|gnames|uworld|fnamepool|processevent/i.test(lowAll)) {{
          a1 = 1.5; ev.a1.push("Unreal Engine 4/5 Reflection (1.5)");
        }} else if (/unityplayer\\.dll|mono_|launchmonodatacollector/i.test(lowAll) || (/\\b(rax|rcx|rdx|r8|r9|r11|movsxd)\\b/i.test(text) && /call\\s+r1[0-5]/i.test(text))) {{
          a1 = 1.15; ev.a1.push("64-bit Unity / Managed JIT Runtime (x64 + JIT trampolines) (1.15)");
        }} else if (/\\b(rax|rcx|rdx|r8|r9|r11|movsxd|qword ptr)\\b/i.test(text)) {{
          a1 = 1.0; ev.a1.push("Native x86_64 Architecture (64-bit registers) (1.0)");
        }} else if (/isaac-ng\\.exe/i.test(lowAll)) {{
          a1 = 0.95; ev.a1.push("Custom C++20 Game Engine (`isaac-ng.exe`) (0.95)");
        }} else if (/engine\\.dll|core\\.dll|render\\.dll/i.test(lowAll)) {{
          a1 = 0.75; ev.a1.push("Multi-module C++ Engine (`Engine.dll` / `Core.dll`) (0.75)");
        }} else if (/popcapgame1\\.exe|plantsvszombies/i.test(lowAll)) {{
          a1 = 0.55; ev.a1.push("32-bit C++ Framework (`popcapgame1.exe` x86) (0.55)");
        }} else {{
          a1 = 0.4; ev.a1.push("Standard 32-bit x86 target (0.4)");
        }}

        if (maxPtrDepth >= 5) {{
          a2 = 1.05; ev.a2.push(`Deep ${{maxPtrDepth}}-level pointer chains (${{offsetBlocks.length}} chains) (1.05)`);
        }} else if (maxPtrDepth >= 3) {{
          a2 = 0.80; ev.a2.push(`Multi-level ${{maxPtrDepth}}-deep pointer chains (0.80)`);
        }} else if (maxContextDisp >= 0x10000 && maxDisp >= 0x1000) {{
          a2 = 1.00; ev.a2.push(`Large C++ engine class offsets (active +0x${{maxDisp.toString(16).toUpperCase()}}, context +0x${{maxContextDisp.toString(16).toUpperCase()}}) (1.00)`);
        }} else if (maxDisp >= 0x1000) {{
          a2 = 0.65; ev.a2.push(`C++ class member offsets (up to +0x${{maxDisp.toString(16).toUpperCase()}}) (0.65)`);
        }} else if (maxPtrDepth >= 1 || maxDisp > 0) {{
          a2 = 0.45; ev.a2.push(`Struct displacement addressing (+0x${{maxDisp.toString(16).toUpperCase()}}) (0.45)`);
        }} else {{
          a2 = 0.20; ev.a2.push("Direct / static addressing (0.20)");
        }}
        if (/mov\\s+\\w+,\\s*\\[[^\\]]+\\][\\s\\S]*?test\\s+\\w+,\\s*\\w+[\\s\\S]*?jz/i.test(activeText)) {{
          a2 = +(Math.min(2.0, a2 + 0.20)).toFixed(2);
          ev.a2.push("Manual NULL-checked pointer walk inside assembly (+0.20)");
        }}

        if (allocCount >= 1) {{
          a3 = 0.65; ev.a3.push(`Auto Assembler codecave hooks (${{allocCount}} allocs, ${{aobCount}} AOB scans) (0.65)`);
        }}
        if (aobCount >= 5) {{
          a3 += 0.25; ev.a3.push(`Multi-feature AOB script suite (${{aobCount}} injections) (+0.25)`);
        }}
        if (aobCount >= 8) {{
          a3 += 0.45; ev.a3.push("Comprehensive 8+ AOB table covering multiple subsystems (+0.45)");
        }}
        if (/check_enemy|enemy_hit|god\\s*mode|one[\\s-]*shot/i.test(activeText) || cmpBranches >= 3) {{
          a3 += 0.30; ev.a3.push("Shared-code entity filter (Player vs Enemy disambiguation) (+0.30)");
        }}
        if (luaCount >= 1) {{
          a3 += 0.20; ev.a3.push(`Embedded Cheat Engine {{$lua}} automation (${{luaCount}} blocks) (+0.20)`);
        }}
        if (nearAlloc64 >= 1) {{
          a3 += 0.35; ev.a3.push(`64-bit RIP-relative near-memory codecaves (${{nearAlloc64}} hooks) (+0.35)`);
        }}
        if (/Float|Double|movss|addss|subss|mulss|xmm/i.test(text)) {{
          a3 += 0.15; ev.a3.push("Floating-point / SSE state manipulation (+0.15)");
        }}
        a3 = +(Math.min(2.0, a3)).toFixed(2);

        if (/vmprotect|themida|arxan|vmp0/i.test(lowAll)) {{
          a4 = 2.0; ev.a4.push("Commercial virtualizer / integrity guard target (2.0)");
        }} else if (aobCount > 0 && nearAlloc64 > 0 && /aobscan\\(/i.test(activeText)) {{
          a4 = 0.55; ev.a4.push("Raw JIT heap AOB scanning (moduleless dynamic CLR/Mono JIT pages) (0.55)");
        }} else if (/\\b(rol|ror|shl|shr|cmov\\w+)\\b/i.test(text)) {{
          a4 = 0.35; ev.a4.push("Bitwise / conditional-move (`cmov`) state logic (0.35)");
        }} else {{
          a4 = 0.0; ev.a4.push("Unobfuscated target binary (0.0)");
        }}

        a5 = 0.0; ev.a5.push("Ring-3 User-Mode Cheat Engine table (0.0)");
        metricsText = `Detected .CT Metrics: AOB Injections = ${{aobCount}} | Codecave Allocs = ${{allocCount}} | Pointer Chains = ${{offsetBlocks.length}} (Max Depth = ${{maxPtrDepth}}) | Max Struct Offset = +0x${{maxDisp.toString(16).toUpperCase()}} | Lua Blocks = ${{luaCount}}`;
      }} else {{
        const clean = text.replace(/\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\/|\"\"\"[\\s\\S]*?\"\"\"/g, "").replace(/\\/\\/[^\\n]*|#[^\\n]*/g, "").toLowerCase();

        if (/remill|mcsema|sleigh|llvm::|irbuilder/.test(clean)) {{ a1 = 2.0; ev.a1.push("Compiler IR / LLVM / SLEIGH lifter (2.0)"); }}
        else if (/jni_getcreatedjavavms|jvmti|cemu|arm64/.test(clean)) {{ a1 = 1.8; ev.a1.push("Cross-ISA / JVM Native Interface (1.8)"); }}
        else if (/il2cpp|gameassembly\\.dll|lua_state/.test(clean)) {{ a1 = 1.6; ev.a1.push("IL2CPP / Embedded Lua VM internals (1.6)"); }}
        else if (/dllmain|virtualprotect|vtable|worldtoscreen|calceulerangles|getprocaddress/.test(clean)) {{ a1 = 1.35; ev.a1.push("Native x64 C++ Engine ABI / VMT / 3D Math (1.35)"); }}
        else if (/harmonypatch|bepinex|dnspy/.test(clean)) {{ a1 = 1.0; ev.a1.push("Managed .NET / Harmony runtime patching (1.0)"); }}
        else if (/openprocess|readprocessmemory|writeprocessmemory|e_lfanew|inspect_pe/.test(clean)) {{ a1 = 0.80; ev.a1.push("Win32 Process & PE Header APIs (0.80)"); }}
        else {{ a1 = 0.50; ev.a1.push("Standard user-mode binary / script (0.50)"); }}

        if (/\\b(cr3|pml4|mmcopyvirtualmemory|eprocess)\\b/.test(clean)) {{ a2 = 2.0; ev.a2.push("x64 CR3 Page Table / Kernel memory walk (2.0)"); }}
        else if (/vtable|vmt|virtualprotect|getprocaddress|fnspawn/.test(clean)) {{ a2 = 1.5; ev.a2.push("In-process function pointer / VMT resolution (1.5)"); }}
        else if (/viewmatrix|worldtoscreen|clipw|ndcx/.test(clean)) {{ a2 = 1.45; ev.a2.push("4x4 View-Projection matrix & clip-space vectors (1.45)"); }}
        else if (/resolvepointerchain|offsets|e_lfanew/.test(clean)) {{ a2 = 0.90; ev.a2.push("Multi-level dynamic pointer chain / PE header walk (0.90)"); }}
        else {{ a2 = 0.40; ev.a2.push("Direct buffer / struct parsing (0.40)"); }}

        if (/z3|solver\\s*\\(\\)|bitvec|angr|claripy|disassemble_crackme06_vm/.test(clean)) {{ a3 = 2.0; ev.a3.push("Automated SMT Theorem Proving (Z3) & Custom VM Disassembler (2.0)"); }}
        else if (/trampoline|minhook|mh_createhook|internalworkerthread/.test(clean)) {{ a3 = 1.85; ev.a3.push("Internal DLL worker thread & native function hook (1.85)"); }}
        else if (/worldtoscreen|calceulerangles|atan2/.test(clean)) {{ a3 = 1.75; ev.a3.push("3D Linear Algebra, Perspective Divide & Aimbot Trig (1.75)"); }}
        else if (/aob|shannon_entropy|math\\.log2/.test(clean)) {{ a3 = 1.40; ev.a3.push("Wildcard AOB scanner & Shannon entropy analyzer (1.40)"); }}
        else if (/readprocessmemory|writeprocessmemory/.test(clean)) {{ a3 = 0.85; ev.a3.push("External Win32 RPM/WPM trainer loop (0.85)"); }}
        else {{ a3 = 0.50; ev.a3.push("Standard control flow logic (0.50)"); }}

        if (/disassemble_crackme06_vm|z3|bitvec|claripy/.test(clean)) {{ a4 = 2.5; ev.a4.push("Custom Stack VM Bytecode & Z3 Symbolic Constraint Solving (2.5)"); }}
        else if (/isdebuggerpresent|ntqueryinformationprocess|rdtsc|shannon_entropy/.test(clean)) {{ a4 = 1.1; ev.a4.push("Anti-Debugging / Packer Entropy / Hash detection (1.1)"); }}
        else if (/rol8|ror8/.test(clean)) {{ a4 = 0.5; ev.a4.push("Bitwise XOR / ROL / ROR cipher inversion (0.5)"); }}
        else {{ a4 = 0.0; ev.a4.push("Unobfuscated target (0.0)"); }}

        if (/iocreatedevice|mmcopyvirtualmemory|memflow/.test(clean)) {{ a5 = 1.5; ev.a5.push("Ring-0 Kernel / KVM VMI boundary (1.5)"); }}
        else if (/disassemble_crackme06_vm/.test(clean)) {{ a5 = 1.4; ev.a5.push("Multi-stage CTF & Symbolic Solver Workbench (1.4)"); }}
        else if (/dllmain|dll_process_attach/.test(clean)) {{ a5 = 1.2; ev.a5.push("In-Process Injected Native DLL (`DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH`) (1.2)"); }}
        else if (/openprocess/.test(clean)) {{ a5 = 0.3; ev.a5.push("Cross-process Win32 Handle (`OpenProcess`) (0.3)"); }}
        else {{ a5 = 0.0; ev.a5.push("Standard Ring-3 utility (0.0)"); }}

        metricsText = `Detected Source/Script Metrics: Lines = ${{text.split("\\n").length}} | File = ${{filename}}`;
      }}

      const total = +(Math.min(10.0, Math.max(0.5, a1 + a2 + a3 + a4 + a5))).toFixed(1);
      const tObj = scoreToTierObj(total);
      return {{ filename, total, tier: tObj.code, tierTitle: tObj.title, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, ev, metricsText }};
    }}

    let activeEngineChip = "";

    function showToast(msg) {{
      const region = document.getElementById("toastRegion");
      if (!region) return;
      const el = document.createElement("div");
      el.className = "toast-msg";
      el.textContent = msg;
      region.appendChild(el);
      setTimeout(() => {{ el.remove(); }}, 3200);
    }}

    function loadSampleArtifact(name) {{
      document.getElementById("gradedFileName").textContent = name;
      document.getElementById("artifactCodeBox").value = SAMPLE_ARTIFACTS[name] || "";
      runAutoGrader();
      showToast(`Loaded verified artifact: ${{name}}`);
    }}

    function handleArtifactUpload(input) {{
      const file = input.files && input.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(e) {{
        document.getElementById("gradedFileName").textContent = file.name;
        document.getElementById("artifactCodeBox").value = e.target.result;
        runAutoGrader();
        showToast(`Analyzed uploaded file: ${{file.name}}`);
      }};
      reader.readAsText(file);
    }}

    // Drag and Drop on #artifactDropzone
    (function initDropzone() {{
      const dz = document.getElementById("artifactDropzone");
      if (!dz) return;
      dz.addEventListener("dragover", e => {{ e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.add("dragover"); }});
      dz.addEventListener("dragleave", () => dz.classList.remove("dragover"));
      dz.addEventListener("drop", e => {{
        e.preventDefault();
        dz.classList.remove("dragover");
        const file = e.dataTransfer && e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = function(ev) {{
          document.getElementById("gradedFileName").textContent = file.name;
          document.getElementById("artifactCodeBox").value = ev.target.result;
          runAutoGrader();
          showToast(`Dropped & analyzed: ${{file.name}}`);
        }};
        reader.readAsText(file);
      }});
    }})();

    function runAutoGrader() {{
      const fname = document.getElementById("gradedFileName").textContent || "artifact.CT";
      const code = document.getElementById("artifactCodeBox").value || "";
      const res = gradeArtifactText(code, fname);
      lastGradedResult = res;

      document.getElementById("gradeTierHeadline").textContent = `Tier ${{res.tier}} (${{res.tierTitle}})`;
      document.getElementById("gradeScoreHeadline").textContent = `${{res.total.toFixed(1)}} / 10.0`;
      document.getElementById("gradeMetricsBox").textContent = res.metricsText;

      const rows = [
        {{ name: "Axis 1: Arch & Runtime", val: res.a1, max: 2.0, desc: res.ev.a1.join(", ") }},
        {{ name: "Axis 2: Memory Indirection", val: res.a2, max: 2.0, desc: res.ev.a2.join(", ") }},
        {{ name: "Axis 3: Code & Hook Logic", val: res.a3, max: 2.0, desc: res.ev.a3.join(", ") }},
        {{ name: "Axis 4: Protection & Obfus.", val: res.a4, max: 2.5, desc: res.ev.a4.join(", ") }},
        {{ name: "Axis 5: Privilege Boundary", val: res.a5, max: 1.5, desc: res.ev.a5.join(", ") }}
      ];
      document.getElementById("gradeAxesBreakdown").innerHTML = rows.map(r => `
        <div class="axis-bar-row">
          <strong style="color:var(--heading);">${{r.name}}</strong>
          <span style="color:var(--gold); font-weight:700; font-family:var(--font-mono);">+${{r.val.toFixed(2)}} / ${{r.max.toFixed(1)}}</span>
          <span style="color:var(--muted);">${{r.desc}}</span>
        </div>
      `).join("");
    }}

    function addGradedArtifactAsQuest() {{
      if (!lastGradedResult) return;
      const newQ = {{
        id: "graded_" + Date.now(),
        tier: lastGradedResult.tier,
        cat: "TOOL",
        kw: lastGradedResult.filename,
        text: `[Auto-Graded ${{lastGradedResult.total.toFixed(1)}}/10] ${{lastGradedResult.filename}} — Verified ${{lastGradedResult.tierTitle}} Artifact`
      }};
      state.customQuests.push(newQ);
      state.checked[newQ.id] = true;
      saveState();
      showToast(`Claimed ${{lastGradedResult.filename}} (Tier ${{lastGradedResult.tier}} | ${{lastGradedResult.total.toFixed(1)}}/10)!`);
    }}

    function setEngineChip(engineKeyword, btn) {{
      activeEngineChip = engineKeyword.toLowerCase();
      document.querySelectorAll(".chip-btn").forEach(b => b.classList.remove("active"));
      if (btn) btn.classList.add("active");
      renderTop200();
    }}

    function renderTop200() {{
      const q = (document.getElementById("top200Search").value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
      const tf = document.getElementById("top200TierFilter").value;
      const tbody = document.getElementById("top200Body");
      if (!tbody) return;

      const groupMap = {{
        NOVICE: ["F+", "E--", "E-", "E", "E+"],
        FRONTIER: ["D-", "D", "D+"],
        MANAGED: ["C-", "C", "C+"],
        AAA: ["B-", "B", "B+"],
        MODERN: ["A-", "A", "A+"],
        ELITE: ["S-", "S", "S+", "SS-", "SS", "SS+", "SSS-", "SSS", "SSS+", "SSS++"]
      }};

      const filtered = TOP200_GAMES.filter(g => {{
        if (tf !== "ALL" && groupMap[tf] && !groupMap[tf].includes(g.tier)) return false;
        if (activeEngineChip && !g.engine.toLowerCase().includes(activeEngineChip) && !g.what_to_find.toLowerCase().includes(activeEngineChip)) return false;
        if (q && !(g.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || g.engine.toLowerCase().includes(q) || g.what_to_find.toLowerCase().includes(q) || g.tier.toLowerCase() === q)) return false;
        return true;
      }});

      tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map(g => {{
        const qid = "top200_" + g.rank;
        const done = !!state.checked[qid];
        return `
          <tr style="${{done ? 'background:var(--green-soft);' : ''}} cursor:pointer;" onclick="toggleQuest('${{qid}}')">
            <td><input type="checkbox" aria-label="Mark ${{g.title}} as completed" ${{done ? 'checked' : ''}} onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleQuest('${{qid}}')" /></td>
            <td><strong style="font-family:var(--font-mono);">#${{g.rank}}</strong></td>
            <th scope="row"><strong style="color:var(--heading);">${{g.title}}</strong></th>
            <td style="color:var(--muted);">${{g.engine}}</td>
            <td><span class="tier-badge">${{g.tier}}</span></td>
            <td style="color:var(--gold); font-weight:700; font-family:var(--font-mono);">${{g.diff}}</td>
            <td>${{g.what_to_find}}</td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }}).join("");
    }}

    function calculateProgression() {{
      const quests = getAllQuests();
      const counts = {{}};
      TIER_META.forEach(t => counts[t.code] = {{ checked: 0, total: 0, tp: 0, cleared: false }});

      let totalChecked = 0;
      let totalExp = 0;
      let dojoDone = 0;
      let top200Done = 0;

      quests.forEach(q => {{
        if (!counts[q.tier]) return;
        counts[q.tier].total++;
        if (state.checked[q.id]) {{
          counts[q.tier].checked++;
          totalChecked++;
          if (q.cat === "DOJO") dojoDone++;
          if (String(q.id).startsWith("top200_")) top200Done++;
          const meta = TIER_META.find(m => m.code === q.tier);
          if (meta) totalExp += meta.exp;
        }}
      }});

      const clearedIndices = [];
      TIER_META.forEach((t, i) => {{
        let samePts = counts[t.code].checked * 1;
        let higherPts = 0;
        for (let j = i + 1; j < TIER_META.length; j++) {{
          higherPts += counts[TIER_META[j].code].checked * 3;
        }}
        counts[t.code].tp = samePts + higherPts;
        if (counts[t.code].tp >= 3) {{
          counts[t.code].cleared = true;
          clearedIndices.push(i);
        }}
      }});

      let curIdx = 0;
      if (clearedIndices.length > 0) {{
        const highestCleared = Math.max(...clearedIndices);
        curIdx = Math.min(TIER_META.length - 1, highestCleared + 1);
      }}

      return {{
        counts,
        totalChecked,
        dojoDone,
        top200Done,
        totalQuests: DEFAULT_QUESTS.length + (state.customQuests || []).length,
        totalExp,
        clearedCount: clearedIndices.length,
        curIdx,
        curTier: TIER_META[curIdx]
      }};
    }}

    function renderCharacterSheet(prog) {{
      document.getElementById("uiRankOrb").textContent = prog.curTier.code;
      document.getElementById("uiClassTitle").textContent =
        `Rank: Tier ${{prog.curTier.code}} — ${{prog.curTier.title}}`;
      document.getElementById("uiTiersCleared").textContent =
        `${{prog.clearedCount}} / ${{TIER_META.length}}`;
      document.getElementById("uiTotalExp").textContent =
        `${{prog.totalExp.toLocaleString()}} EXP`;
      document.getElementById("uiQuestsDone").textContent =
        `${{prog.totalChecked}}`;

      const pct = Math.round((prog.clearedCount / TIER_META.length) * 100);
      document.getElementById("uiCampaignBar").style.width = `${{pct}}%`;
      const track = document.getElementById("uiProgressTrack");
      if (track) track.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(pct));

      const curTp = Math.min(3, prog.counts[prog.curTier.code].tp);
      document.getElementById("uiTrialLabel").textContent =
        `Tier ${{prog.curTier.code}} Rank-Up Trial (3 Same-Tier OR 1 Higher-Tier Breakthrough):`;
      document.getElementById("uiTpStatus").textContent = `${{curTp}} / 3 TP`;

      const nextTierObj = TIER_META[Math.min(TIER_META.length - 1, prog.curIdx + 1)];
      const elDojo = document.getElementById("teleDojoDone");
      const elTop = document.getElementById("teleTop200Done");
      const elNext = document.getElementById("teleNextRank");
      if (elDojo) elDojo.textContent = `${{prog.dojoDone}} / 18`;
      if (elTop) elTop.textContent = `${{prog.top200Done}} / 200`;
      if (elNext) elNext.textContent = nextTierObj ? nextTierObj.code : "MAX";

      // Update 5-Axis SVG Capability Radar
      const poly = document.getElementById("radarDataPoly");
      if (poly) {{
        const ratio = Math.max(0.18, Math.min(1.0, (prog.curIdx + 2) / TIER_META.length));
        const rArch = Math.min(1.0, ratio * 1.15);
        const rPtr  = Math.min(1.0, ratio * 1.10);
        const rHook = Math.min(1.0, ratio * 1.05);
        const rObf  = Math.min(1.0, Math.max(0.15, ratio * 0.85));
        const rRing = Math.min(1.0, Math.max(0.15, ratio * 0.75));
        const p1 = `110,${{(95 - 75 * rArch).toFixed(1)}}`;
        const p2 = `${{(110 + 80 * rPtr).toFixed(1)}},${{(95 - 20 * rPtr).toFixed(1)}}`;
        const p3 = `${{(110 + 50 * rHook).toFixed(1)}},${{(95 + 67 * rHook).toFixed(1)}}`;
        const p4 = `${{(110 - 50 * rObf).toFixed(1)}},${{(95 + 67 * rObf).toFixed(1)}}`;
        const p5 = `${{(110 - 80 * rRing).toFixed(1)}},${{(95 - 20 * rRing).toFixed(1)}}`;
        poly.setAttribute("points", `${{p1}} ${{p2}} ${{p3}} ${{p4}} ${{p5}}`);
      }}

      const skills = [
        {{ name: "Stage 1: Memory & Bitmasks", ok: prog.totalChecked >= 1 }},
        {{ name: "Stage 2: Pointers & AutoAsm", ok: prog.curIdx >= 5 }},
        {{ name: "Stage 3: Shared Code Filters", ok: prog.curIdx >= 8 }},
        {{ name: "Stage 4: Unity Mono & .NET", ok: prog.curIdx >= 11 }},
        {{ name: "Stage 5: C++ VMT & DLL Hooks", ok: prog.curIdx >= 14 }},
        {{ name: "Stage 6: UE4/5 SDK & 3D W2S", ok: prog.curIdx >= 16 }},
        {{ name: "Stage 7: IL2CPP & Custom VMs", ok: prog.curIdx >= 18 }},
        {{ name: "Stage 8: Z3, O-LLVM & VMI", ok: prog.curIdx >= 25 }}
      ];
      document.getElementById("uiSkillGrid").innerHTML = skills.map(s =>
        `<div class="skill-chip ${{s.ok ? 'unlocked' : ''}}">${{s.ok ? '✅' : '🔒'}} ${{s.name}}</div>`
      ).join("");
    }}

    function renderTiers() {{
      const prog = calculateProgression();
      renderCharacterSheet(prog);

      const query = document.getElementById("searchBox").value.toLowerCase().trim();
      const catF = document.getElementById("catFilter").value;
      const statF = document.getElementById("statusFilter").value;
      const quests = DEFAULT_QUESTS.concat(state.customQuests || []);

      const container = document.getElementById("tiersContainer");
      container.innerHTML = "";

      let renderedIdx = 0;
      TIER_META.forEach(t => {{
        const c = prog.counts[t.code];
        if (statF === "LOCKED" && c.cleared) return;
        if (statF === "CLEARED" && !c.cleared) return;

        const tierQuests = quests.filter(q => {{
          if (q.tier !== t.code) return false;
          if (catF !== "ALL" && q.cat !== catF) return false;
          if (query && !q.text.toLowerCase().includes(query) && !t.title.toLowerCase().includes(query)) return false;
          return true;
        }});

        if (tierQuests.length === 0) return;

        const tpDisplay = Math.min(3, c.tp);
        const card = document.createElement("div");
        // Apply deferred-tier (content-visibility: auto + contain-intrinsic-size) ONLY to off-screen cards below the initial fold
        const deferClass = renderedIdx >= 3 ? "deferred-tier" : "";
        renderedIdx++;
        card.className = `tier-card ${{c.cleared ? 'cleared' : ''}} ${{deferClass}}`.trim();
        card.innerHTML = `
          <div class="tier-top">
            <div>
              <span class="tier-badge">Tier ${{t.code}}</span>
              <strong style="margin-left:8px; color:var(--heading);">${{t.title}}</strong>
              <span style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.8rem; margin-left:8px;">(${{t.act}} • Diff ${{t.diff.toFixed(1)}}/10 • +${{t.exp}} EXP)</span>
            </div>
            <span class="tp-pill" style="color:${{c.cleared ? 'var(--green-bright)' : 'var(--gold)'}}">
              ${{c.cleared ? '✅ TIER CLEARED' : `⚡ ${{tpDisplay}}/3 TP`}}
            </span>
          </div>
          <ul class="quest-list">
            ${{tierQuests.map(q => {{
              const isDone = !!state.checked[q.id];
              const tagClass = q.cat === "DOJO" ? "tag-dojo" : q.cat === "GAME" ? "tag-game" : q.cat === "SOFT" ? "tag-soft" : "tag-tool";
              const tagLabel = q.cat === "DOJO" ? "🧪 Dojo Lab" : q.cat === "GAME" ? "🎮 Game" : q.cat === "SOFT" ? "💻 Software" : "🛠️ Toolcraft";
              return `
                <li class="quest-item ${{isDone ? 'done' : ''}}" onclick="toggleQuest('${{q.id}}')">
                  <input type="checkbox" aria-label="${{q.text}}" ${{isDone ? 'checked' : ''}} onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleQuest('${{q.id}}')" />
                  <span class="q-tag ${{tagClass}}">${{tagLabel}}</span>
                  <span style="flex:1; font-size:0.86rem;">${{q.text}}</span>
                </li>
              `;
            }}).join("")}}
          </ul>
        `;
        container.appendChild(card);
      }});

      renderTop200();
      renderScoreboard(prog);
    }}

    function toggleQuest(id) {{
      state.checked[id] = !state.checked[id];
      if (!state.checked[id]) delete state.checked[id];
      saveState();
    }}

    function renderScoreboard(prog) {{
      const tbody = document.getElementById("scoreboardBody");
      const userName = state.playerName || "New Challenger";
      tbody.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td>🥇 <strong>#1</strong></td>
          <th scope="row"><strong style="color:var(--heading);">deavers</strong> <span style="color:var(--accent-bright); font-size:0.75rem;">[Author / Verified]</span></th>
          <td>Adventurer (Managed JIT)</td>
          <td><span class="tier-badge">Tier C</span></td>
          <td style="color:var(--gold); font-weight:700; font-family:var(--font-mono);">6,850 EXP</td>
          <td><code>PvZ.CT</code> (2.1 E+), <code>UnrealTournament_UT99.CT</code> (2.9 D), <code>isaac-ng.CT</code> (3.8 C-), <code>StreetsOfRogue_v98.CT</code> (4.1 C)</td>
        </tr>
        <tr style="background:var(--accent-soft);">
          <td>⚡ <strong>YOU</strong></td>
          <th scope="row"><strong style="color:var(--heading);">${{userName}}</strong> <span style="color:var(--green-bright); font-size:0.75rem;">[Browser Session]</span></th>
          <td>${{prog.curTier.title}}</td>
          <td><span class="tier-badge">Tier ${{prog.curTier.code}}</span></td>
          <td style="color:var(--gold); font-weight:700; font-family:var(--font-mono);">${{prog.totalExp.toLocaleString()}} EXP</td>
          <td>${{prog.totalChecked}} Quests Checked</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }}

    function exportProgressJson() {{
      const prog = calculateProgression();
      const allQ = getAllQuests();
      const completedKeywords = allQ.filter(q => state.checked[q.id] && q.kw).map(q => q.kw);
      const payload = {{
        player_name: state.playerName,
        rank_label: `Tier ${{prog.curTier.code}} (${{prog.curTier.title}})`,
        current_tier: prog.curTier.code,
        total_exp: prog.totalExp,
        quests_completed: prog.totalChecked,
        completed_keywords: completedKeywords,
        browser_state: state
      }};
      const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], {{ type: "application/json" }});
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = "player_progress.json";
      a.click();
      showToast("Exported player_progress.json");
    }}

    function importProgressJson(input) {{
      const file = input.files && input.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(e) {{
        try {{
          const data = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
          if (data.browser_state) {{
            state = Object.assign(state, data.browser_state);
          }} else if (data.player_name) {{
            state.playerName = data.player_name;
          }}
          document.getElementById("uiPlayerName").value = state.playerName;
          saveState();
          showToast("Profile imported successfully!");
        }} catch (err) {{
          showToast("Error reading JSON file");
        }}
      }};
      reader.readAsText(file);
    }}

    function applyDeaversPreset() {{
      state.playerName = "deavers";
      state.checked = {{}};
      DEFAULT_QUESTS.forEach(q => {{ if (q.deavers) state.checked[q.id] = true; }});
      document.getElementById("uiPlayerName").value = "deavers";
      saveState();
      showToast("Loaded verified deavers preset (Tier C | 6,850 EXP)");
    }}

    function resetProfile() {{
      state.playerName = "New Challenger";
      state.checked = {{}};
      state.customQuests = [];
      document.getElementById("uiPlayerName").value = "New Challenger";
      saveState();
      showToast("Reset profile to Tier F- (0 EXP)");
    }}

    function recalcAndRender() {{
      renderTiers();
    }}

    // Same-Document View Transition + Accessible ARIA Tab Selection & Focus Routing
    function switchTab(name, btn, focusTargetHeading = false) {{
      const applyDomSwitch = () => {{
        document.querySelectorAll(".tab-panel").forEach(p => p.classList.remove("active"));
        document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach(b => {{
          b.classList.remove("active");
          b.setAttribute("aria-selected", "false");
        }});
        const targetPanel = document.getElementById("tab-" + name);
        if (targetPanel) targetPanel.classList.add("active");
        const activeBtn = btn || document.getElementById("tabbtn-" + name);
        if (activeBtn) {{
          activeBtn.classList.add("active");
          activeBtn.setAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
        }}
      }};

      const routeFocus = () => {{
        if (!focusTargetHeading) return;
        const heading = document.getElementById("heading-" + name);
        if (heading) heading.focus({{ preventScroll: true }});
      }};

      if (!document.startViewTransition) {{
        applyDomSwitch();
        routeFocus();
        return;
      }}
      const transition = document.startViewTransition(() => {{
        applyDomSwitch();
      }});
      transition.finished.then(routeFocus);
    }}

    // Command Palette (Ctrl+K / Cmd+K)
    function openCmdPalette() {{
      const dlg = document.getElementById("cmdDialog");
      if (!dlg) return;
      if (!dlg.open) dlg.showModal();
      const inp = document.getElementById("cmdSearchInput");
      inp.value = "";
      renderCmdResults();
      inp.focus();
    }}

    function closeCmdPalette() {{
      const dlg = document.getElementById("cmdDialog");
      if (dlg && dlg.open) dlg.close();
    }}

    function renderCmdResults() {{
      const q = (document.getElementById("cmdSearchInput").value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
      const list = document.getElementById("cmdResultsList");
      if (!list) return;

      const actions = [
        {{ label: "⚔️ Go to 28-Tier Campaign Quests", sub: "Navigation", act: () => switchTab("quests", null, true) }},
        {{ label: "🔥 Go to Top 200 Popular Games (#001–#200)", sub: "Navigation", act: () => switchTab("top200", null, true) }},
        {{ label: "🔬 Go to Automated .CT / Script Grader", sub: "Navigation", act: () => switchTab("autograde", null, true) }},
        {{ label: "📖 Go to x86/x64 & Engine Cheat Sheet", sub: "Navigation", act: () => switchTab("cheatsheet", null, true) }},
        {{ label: "⚖️ Go to 5-Axis Target Calculator", sub: "Navigation", act: () => switchTab("adder", null, true) }},
        {{ label: "🧙‍♂️ Load Verified Author Preset (deavers — Tier C)", sub: "Preset", act: () => applyDeaversPreset() }},
        {{ label: "🌱 Reset Profile to Clean New Game (Tier F-)", sub: "Preset", act: () => resetProfile() }}
      ];

      TOP200_GAMES.slice(0, 60).forEach(g => {{
        actions.push({{
          label: `🎮 #${{g.rank}} ${{g.title}} (${{g.engine}})`,
          sub: `Tier ${{g.tier}} • ${{g.diff}}`,
          act: () => {{
            switchTab("top200", null, true);
            const sb = document.getElementById("top200Search");
            if (sb) {{ sb.value = g.title; renderTop200(); }}
          }}
        }});
      }});

      const matches = actions.filter(a => !q || a.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) || a.sub.toLowerCase().includes(q)).slice(0, 12);
      list.innerHTML = matches.map((m, idx) => `
        <li class="cmd-item" tabindex="0" data-cmd-idx="${{idx}}">
          <span>${{m.label}}</span>
          <span class="tier-badge">${{m.sub}}</span>
        </li>
      `).join("");

      list.querySelectorAll(".cmd-item").forEach(el => {{
        const idx = Number(el.getAttribute("data-cmd-idx"));
        el.onclick = () => {{ closeCmdPalette(); matches[idx].act(); }};
        el.onkeydown = (ev) => {{
          if (ev.key === "Enter" || ev.key === " ") {{
            ev.preventDefault();
            closeCmdPalette();
            matches[idx].act();
          }}
        }};
      }});
    }}

    document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {{
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {{
        e.preventDefault();
        openCmdPalette();
      }}
    }});

    loadState();
    updateRubricPreview();
    loadSampleArtifact("PvZ.CT");
    recalcAndRender();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
"""

(ROOT / "docs" / "index.html").write_text(html, encoding="utf-8")
print(f"[+] Generated 100%-English docs/index.html ({len(html):,} bytes)!")

`Tools_and_Scripts/opsec_sanitizer.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
opsec_sanitizer.py — Automated OPSEC, Privacy, Binary & Git History Auditor
=============================================================================
Protects the repository author and contributors by scanning:
  1. All tracked/untracked workspace files for OS paths (C:\\Users\\..., /home/...),
     personal emails, Windows hostnames (DESKTOP-..., WIN-...), or leaked tokens.
  2. All compiled PE binaries (.exe / .dll) in build/ to ensure /PDBALTPATH:%_PDB%
     stripped local C:\\Users\\... paths from the RSDS debug directory.
  3. Git staging status to block forbidden artifacts (*.dmp, *.pdb, *.ilk, *.i64).
  4. Local Git config & commit history (git log) to ensure all commits use the
     anonymous handle ('deavers') and GitHub noreply email.

Usage:
  python Tools_and_Scripts/opsec_sanitizer.py
  python Tools_and_Scripts/opsec_sanitizer.py --fix-git
  python Tools_and_Scripts/opsec_sanitizer.py --ci
"""

import argparse
import os
import pathlib
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

ALLOWED_EMAIL_DOMAINS = {
    "users.noreply.github.com",
    "noreply.github.com",
    "github.com",
    "example.com",
}

FORBIDDEN_TRACKED_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".dmp", ".mdmp", ".hdmp", ".core",
    ".pdb", ".ilk", ".idb", ".i64", ".bndb", ".dd32", ".dd64",
    ".pfx", ".pem", ".key",
}

PATH_PATTERNS = [
    (re.compile(r"[A-Za-z]:\\[Uu]sers\\[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+"), "Windows Local User Path"),
    (re.compile(r"[A-Za-z]:/[Uu]sers/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+"), "Windows Local User Path (Forward Slash)"),
    (re.compile(r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9])/(?:home|Users)/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+"), "Unix/macOS Local Home Path"),
    (re.compile(r"\\AppData\\(?:Local|Roaming|LocalLow)\\", re.IGNORECASE), "Windows AppData Path"),
    (re.compile(r"\b(?:DESKTOP|LAPTOP)-[A-Z0-9]{5,15}\b"), "Windows Hostname Identifier"),
    (re.compile(r"(?:ghp|gho|ghu|ghs|ghr)_[A-Za-z0-9_]{30,255}"), "GitHub Personal Access Token"),
]

EMAIL_REGEX = re.compile(r"\b([A-Za-z0-9_.+-]+)@([A-Za-z0-9-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9-.]+)\b")


def scan_workspace_files() -> list[str]:
    issues = []
    text_exts = {
        ".md", ".html", ".json", ".py", ".cpp", ".hpp", ".h", ".c",
        ".CT", ".ct", ".bat", ".sh", ".ps1", ".yml", ".yaml", ".txt", ".cmake"
    }

    for p in sorted(ROOT.rglob("*")):
        if not p.is_file():
            continue
        if ".git" in p.parts or "__pycache__" in p.parts:
            continue
        rel = p.relative_to(ROOT)

        # Check binary files in build/ for embedded C:\Users\ strings
        if "build" in p.parts:
            if p.suffix.lower() in {".exe", ".dll", ".pdb", ".ilk"}:
                raw = p.read_bytes()
                if b"\\Users\\" in raw or b"/Users/" in raw:
                    issues.append(
                        f"[BINARY PATH LEAK] {rel} contains embedded '\\Users\\' path! "
                        f"Rebuild with /PDBALTPATH:%_PDB% or clean build/ folder."
                    )
            continue

        if p.suffix in text_exts or p.name in {"LICENSE", ".gitignore", ".gitattributes", "CMakeLists.txt"}:
            # Skip self-scanning regex definitions in opsec_sanitizer.py and SECURITY.md examples
            if p.name in {"opsec_sanitizer.py", "SECURITY.md"}:
                continue

            txt = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
            for idx, line in enumerate(txt.splitlines(), 1):
                for rx, label in PATH_PATTERNS:
                    m = rx.search(line)
                    if m:
                        issues.append(f"[FILE LEAK: {label}] {rel}:{idx} -> '{m.group(0)}'")

                for em in EMAIL_REGEX.finditer(line):
                    full_email = em.group(0)
                    domain = em.group(2).lower()
                    # Ignore false-positive assembly symbols or decorator strings
                    if domain not in ALLOWED_EMAIL_DOMAINS and "." in domain:
                        issues.append(f"[PERSONAL EMAIL LEAK] {rel}:{idx} -> '{full_email}'")

    return issues


def check_git_tracked_files() -> list[str]:
    issues = []
    try:
        out = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "status", "--porcelain"], cwd=ROOT, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL
        )
        for line in out.splitlines():
            if len(line) < 4:
                continue
            filepath = line[3:].strip().strip('"')
            ext = pathlib.Path(filepath).suffix.lower()
            if ext in FORBIDDEN_TRACKED_EXTENSIONS:
                issues.append(f"[FORBIDDEN ARTIFACT] '{filepath}' ({ext}) must never be committed!")
    except Exception:
        pass
    return issues


def check_git_identity_and_history(ci_mode: bool = False) -> list[str]:
    issues = []
    if not (ROOT / ".git").exists():
        return issues

    if not ci_mode:
        try:
            uname = subprocess.check_output(
                ["git", "config", "user.name"], cwd=ROOT, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL
            ).strip()
            uemail = subprocess.check_output(
                ["git", "config", "user.email"], cwd=ROOT, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL
            ).strip()
            if uname != "deavers":
                issues.append(f"[GIT CONFIG] Local git user.name is '{uname}' (recommended: 'deavers').")
            if not uemail.endswith("@users.noreply.github.com"):
                issues.append(
                    f"[GIT CONFIG] Local git user.email is '{uemail}' "
                    f"(recommended: '106682353+deavers@users.noreply.github.com')."
                )
        except Exception:
            issues.append("[GIT CONFIG] Local git user.name / user.email not configured.")

    try:
        log_out = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "log", "--format=%h|%an|%ae|%cn|%ce"], cwd=ROOT, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL
        )
        for line in log_out.splitlines():
            parts = line.split("|")
            if len(parts) != 5:
                continue
            commit_hash, an, ae, cn, ce = parts
            for email_val, role in [(ae, "Author"), (ce, "Committer")]:
                domain = email_val.split("@")[-1].lower() if "@" in email_val else ""
                if domain not in ALLOWED_EMAIL_DOMAINS:
                    issues.append(
                        f"[GIT HISTORY EMAIL LEAK] Commit {commit_hash} exposes {role} email '{email_val}'"
                    )
            for name_val, role in [(an, "Author"), (cn, "Committer")]:
                if name_val.lower() not in {"deavers", "github", "github-actions[bot]"}:
                    issues.append(
                        f"[GIT HISTORY NAME LEAK] Commit {commit_hash} exposes {role} name '{name_val}' (expected 'deavers')"
                    )
    except Exception:
        pass

    return issues


def fix_git_config_and_history() -> None:
    print("[*] Setting local Git author identity to 'deavers <106682353+deavers@users.noreply.github.com>'...")
    subprocess.run(["git", "config", "--local", "user.name", "deavers"], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    subprocess.run(
        ["git", "config", "--local", "user.email", "106682353+deavers@users.noreply.github.com"],
        cwd=ROOT,
        check=True,
    )

    # Clean build directory if it contains old unstripped binaries
    build_dir = ROOT / "build"
    if build_dir.exists():
        print("[*] Removing local build/ directory to purge old unstripped PE/PDB artifacts...")
        shutil.rmtree(build_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    # Rewrite git history in-place (without touching working tree) so all personal names/emails become 'deavers'
    print("[*] Scrubbing Git commit history so all personal names/emails become 'deavers'...")
    revs = subprocess.check_output(
        ["git", "rev-list", "--reverse", "--topo-order", "HEAD"], cwd=ROOT, text=True
    ).splitlines()

    commit_map: dict[str, str] = {}
    bot_names = {"github-actions[bot]"}
    anon_ident = "deavers <106682353+deavers@users.noreply.github.com>"

    for old_sha in revs:
        raw_commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "cat-file", "commit", old_sha], cwd=ROOT)
        header_bytes, sep, body_bytes = raw_commit.partition(b"\n\n")
        header_lines = header_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()
        new_header_lines = []

        for hline in header_lines:
            if hline.startswith("parent "):
                old_parent = hline.split(" ", 1)[1].strip()
                new_parent = commit_map.get(old_parent, old_parent)
                new_header_lines.append(f"parent {new_parent}")
            elif hline.startswith("author "):
                # Format: author Name <email> timestamp tz
                m = re.match(r"^author (.+?) <(.+?)> (\d+ [+-]\d{4})$", hline)
                if m and m.group(1) not in bot_names:
                    new_header_lines.append(f"author {anon_ident} {m.group(3)}")
                else:
                    new_header_lines.append(hline)
            elif hline.startswith("committer "):
                m = re.match(r"^committer (.+?) <(.+?)> (\d+ [+-]\d{4})$", hline)
                if m and m.group(1) not in (bot_names | {"GitHub"}):
                    new_header_lines.append(f"committer {anon_ident} {m.group(3)}")
                else:
                    new_header_lines.append(hline)
            else:
                new_header_lines.append(hline)

        new_raw = ("\n".join(new_header_lines)).encode("utf-8") + sep + body_bytes
        new_sha = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "hash-object", "-t", "commit", "-w", "--stdin"],
            cwd=ROOT,
            input=new_raw,
        ).decode("utf-8").strip()
        commit_map[old_sha] = new_sha

    if revs and revs[-1] in commit_map:
        new_head = commit_map[revs[-1]]
        head_ref = subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "symbolic-ref", "HEAD"], cwd=ROOT, text=True
        ).strip()
        subprocess.run(["git", "update-ref", head_ref, new_head], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
        for remote_ref in ["refs/remotes/origin/main", "refs/remotes/origin/HEAD"]:
            try:
                subprocess.run(["git", "update-ref", "-d", remote_ref], cwd=ROOT, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            except Exception:
                pass

    subprocess.run(["git", "reflog", "expire", "--expire=now", "--all"], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "gc", "--prune=now", "--quiet"], cwd=ROOT, check=True)
    print("[+] Git history and local config successfully anonymized!")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="OPSEC, PII & Binary Path Sanitizer")
    parser.add_argument("--fix-git", action="store_true", help="Anonymize local git config, history, and clean build/")
    parser.add_argument("--ci", action="store_true", help="Run in GitHub Actions CI mode")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.fix_git:
        fix_git_config_and_history()

    print("=" * 74)
    print("  [OPSEC SHIELD] REVERSE ENGINEERING JOURNEY - PRIVACY SANITIZER AUDIT")
    print("=" * 74)

    file_issues = scan_workspace_files()
    tracked_issues = check_git_tracked_files()
    git_issues = check_git_identity_and_history(ci_mode=args.ci)

    all_issues = file_issues + tracked_issues + git_issues

    if not all_issues:
        print("  [PASS] 0 Personal Paths (C:\\Users\\...) found in workspace files.")
        print("  [PASS] 0 Personal Email Addresses or Hostnames found.")
        print("  [PASS] 0 Unstripped PE/PDB Binaries or Memory Dumps found.")
        print("  [PASS] Git Commit History & Local Config 100% Anonymized ('deavers').")
        print("=" * 74)
        sys.exit(0)
    else:
        print(f"  [WARNING] Found {len(all_issues)} potential OPSEC/Privacy issue(s):\n")
        for idx, issue in enumerate(all_issues, 1):
            print(f"    {idx:02d}. {issue}")
        print("\n  Tip: Run 'python Tools_and_Scripts/opsec_sanitizer.py --fix-git' to auto-fix!")
        print("=" * 74)
        sys.exit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`Tools_and_Scripts/rpg_manager.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
============================================================================
[RPG Manager] Auto-Updating Character Card, Breakthrough Level-Up & Rubric DB
============================================================================
FEATURES:
  1. Parses all 28 Tiers (`F-` -> `SSS++`) and `- [ ]` / `- [x]` checkboxes
     in `README.md`.
  2. Applies the Smart "Breakthrough" Level-Up Rule:
       - Clearing a Tier requires 3 Tier Points (3 TP).
       - Each quest completed in Tier T gives +1 TP to Tier T.
       - BREAKTHROUGH: Each quest completed in a higher Tier (T+1 or above)
         automatically grants +3 TP to Tier T (instantly clearing Tier T!).
  3. 5-Axis Anti-Inflation Rubric Calculator:
       - Reads `data/custom_targets.json`, computes the objective Difficulty
         Score (0.1 to 10.0) and RPG Tier from 5 technical axes (preventing
         arbitrary "SSS++" inflation), and renders them into `README.md`.
  4. Modes:
       - Default: Recalculate RPG Card from current checkboxes in `README.md`
       - `--name "Handle"` : Set player name on the RPG Card
       - `--reset`         : Reset all checkboxes to `- [ ]` (Clean New Game)
       - `--preset deavers`: Load `deavers`'s completed milestones
       - `--add-target`    : Interactive 5-Axis Rubric CLI wizard
============================================================================
"""

import argparse
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import quote

ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
README_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "README.md"
CUSTOM_DB_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "data" / "custom_targets.json"
TOP100_DB_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "data" / "top200_popular_games.json"
PROGRESS_JSON_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "data" / "player_progress.json"

TIERS = [
    ("F-",    0.2,  "White-Box Novice",         100),
    ("F",     0.4,  "Memory Initiate",          120),
    ("F+",    0.6,  "State Scavenger",          150),
    ("E--",   1.0,  "Tutorial Apprentice",      180),
    ("E-",    1.4,  "Win32 Tinkerer",           220),
    ("E",     1.8,  "Pointer Scout",            260),
    ("E+",    2.2,  "Code-Cave Squire",         300),
    ("D-",    2.6,  "Sector Ranger",            350),
    ("D",     3.0,  "Arena Vanguard",           400),
    ("D+",    3.4,  "Memory Tracker",           450),
    ("C-",    3.8,  "Struct Alchemist",         520),
    ("C",     4.2,  "Adventurer (Mono/CLR)",    600),
    ("C+",    4.6,  "Harmony Hexer",            680),
    ("B-",    5.0,  "RTTI Knight",              770),
    ("B",     5.4,  "VMT Paladin",              860),
    ("B+",    5.8,  "Internal Dragoon",         960),
    ("A-",    6.2,  "Unreal Architect",        1100),
    ("A",     6.6,  "IL2CPP Inquisitor",       1250),
    ("A+",    7.0,  "Hybrid VM Warlock",       1400),
    ("S-",    7.4,  "Soulslike Sage",          1600),
    ("S",     7.7,  "DOD Grandmaster",         1800),
    ("S+",    8.0,  "Cross-Arch Empath",       2000),
    ("SS-",   8.3,  "Protocol Cryptographer",  2250),
    ("SS",    8.6,  "Unpacker Sovereign",      2500),
    ("SS+",   8.9,  "VM Slayer",               2800),
    ("SSS-",  9.2,  "Symbolic Oracle (Z3)",    3200),
    ("SSS",   9.5,  "Ring-0 / VMI Phantom",    3600),
    ("SSS+",  9.8,  "Decompilation Titan",     4200),
    ("SSS++", 10.0, "Demigod of Silicon",      5000),
]

DEAVERS_PRESET_KEYWORDS = [
    "lab01_static_scalars",
    "lab02_datatypes_bitmasks",
    "lab03_grid_minesweeper",
    "lab04_pointer_chains",
    "lab05_code_injection",
    "lab06_shared_code",
    "lab07_vtable_reclass",
    "crackme01_strings",
    "crackme02_serial_math",
    "crackme03_xor_rol",
    "crackme04_binary_license",
    "Plants vs. Zombies GOTY",
    "Unreal Tournament 99",
    "The Binding of Isaac: Repentance",
    "Streets of Rogue (v98)",
]


def score_to_tier(score: float) -> str:
    score = max(0.1, min(10.0, round(score, 1)))
    best_tier = "F-"
    for code, max_diff, _, _ in TIERS:
        best_tier = code
        if score <= max_diff + 0.15:
            break
    return best_tier


def compute_rubric_score(rubric: dict) -> tuple[float, str]:
    arch = min(2.0, max(0.1, float(rubric.get("arch_symbols", 0.2))))
    mem = min(2.0, max(0.0, float(rubric.get("memory_indirection", 0.1))))
    eng = min(2.0, max(0.0, float(rubric.get("engine_complexity", 0.1))))
    prot = min(2.5, max(0.0, float(rubric.get("binary_protection", 0.0))))
    priv = min(1.5, max(0.0, float(rubric.get("privilege_boundary", 0.0))))
    total = round(min(10.0, max(0.1, arch + mem + eng + prot + priv)), 1)
    return total, score_to_tier(total)


def render_custom_targets_section() -> str:
    if not CUSTOM_DB_PATH.exists():
        return ""
    data = json.loads(CUSTOM_DB_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    targets = data.get("targets", [])
    lines = [
        "<!-- CUSTOM_TARGETS_START -->",
        "| Status | Computed Tier | Rubric Score | Category | Target Title | Engine / Arch | Quest Objective |",
        "| :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- |",
    ]
    for t in targets:
        score, tier = compute_rubric_score(t.get("rubric", {}))
        chk = "[x]" if t.get("completed", False) else "[ ]"
        lines.append(
            f"| - {chk} | **{tier}** | `{score:.1f} / 10` | {t.get('category', 'Game')} | **{t.get('title', 'Custom')}** | {t.get('engine', 'N/A')} | {t.get('quest', '')} |"
        )
    lines.append("<!-- CUSTOM_TARGETS_END -->")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_top100_section() -> str:
    if not TOP100_DB_PATH.exists():
        return ""
    top200 = json.loads(TOP100_DB_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    lines = [
        "<!-- TOP100_GAMES_START -->",
        f"## 🔥 Top {len(top200)} Most Popular & Iconic Games for Reverse Engineering (`#001` – `#{len(top200):03d}`)",
        "",
        f"Complete reference guide of the **Top {len(top200)} most popular & iconic offline/single-player PC games** (sorted by difficulty from `0.5 / 10` to `9.8 / 10`), detailing each game's **Engine & Architecture**, **RPG Tier**, **Difficulty**, and **What to Search & Reverse (in-memory structs, pointer chains, classes, VMTs, matrices, and functions)**:",
        "",
        "| # | Game Title | Engine & Architecture | Tier | Difficulty | 🎯 What to Search & Reverse (Structs, Offsets, Classes & Hooks) |",
        "| :-: | :--- | :--- | :-: | :-: | :--- |",
    ]
    for g in top200:
        r_str = g["rank"] if isinstance(g["rank"], str) else f"{int(g['rank']):03d}"
        lines.append(
            f"| **{r_str}** | **{g['title']}** | {g['engine']} | **{g['tier']}** | `{g['diff']}` | {g['what_to_find']} |"
        )
    lines.append("<!-- TOP100_GAMES_END -->")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def parse_tier_checkboxes(md_text: str) -> dict:
    # Split by tier headers: ### ... [TIER_CODE] ...
    tier_counts = {code: {"checked": 0, "total": 0} for code, _, _, _ in TIERS}
    header_pattern = re.compile(r"^###\s+.*?Tier\s+`([A-Z\+\-]+)`", re.MULTILINE)
    matches = list(header_pattern.finditer(md_text))

    for idx, m in enumerate(matches):
        tier_code = m.group(1)
        start = m.end()
        end = matches[idx + 1].start() if idx + 1 < len(matches) else len(md_text)
        block = md_text[start:end]

        checked = len(re.findall(r"^\s*-\s*\[[xX]\]", block, re.MULTILINE))
        unchecked = len(re.findall(r"^\s*-\s*\[\s\]", block, re.MULTILINE))

        if tier_code == "F-":
            # Also handle combined F- & F header if present
            pass
        if tier_code in tier_counts:
            tier_counts[tier_code]["checked"] += checked
            tier_counts[tier_code]["total"] += checked + unchecked

    return tier_counts


def calculate_progression(tier_counts: dict) -> dict:
    total_checked = sum(v["checked"] for v in tier_counts.values())
    total_boxes = sum(v["total"] for v in tier_counts.values())

    total_exp = 0
    for code, _, _, exp_per_quest in TIERS:
        total_exp += tier_counts[code]["checked"] * exp_per_quest

    # Breakthrough Level-Up Rule:
    # A Tier T is cleared if:
    #   TP(T) = checked(T)*1 + sum_{k > T} checked(k)*3 >= 3
    cleared_indices = []
    tier_tp = {}
    for i, (code, _, _, _) in enumerate(TIERS):
        same_tier_pts = tier_counts[code]["checked"] * 1
        higher_tier_pts = sum(tier_counts[TIERS[j][0]]["checked"] * 3 for j in range(i + 1, len(TIERS)))
        tp = same_tier_pts + higher_tier_pts
        tier_tp[code] = tp
        if tp >= 3:
            cleared_indices.append(i)

    if not cleared_indices and total_checked == 0:
        current_idx = 0
        rank_label = "Tier F- (Unranked Novice)"
        class_title = "White-Box Novice"
        max_diff = 0.0
    else:
        highest_cleared = max(cleared_indices) if cleared_indices else -1
        # Clearing Tier i (>= 3 TP) promotes the Adventurer to Tier i + 1!
        current_idx = min(len(TIERS) - 1, highest_cleared + 1)
        code, max_diff, class_title, _ = TIERS[current_idx]
        rank_label = f"Tier {code} ({class_title})"

    next_idx = min(len(TIERS) - 1, current_idx + 1)
    next_code, _, next_title, _ = TIERS[next_idx]
    cur_tp = min(3, tier_tp[TIERS[current_idx][0]])

    return {
        "total_checked": total_checked,
        "total_boxes": max(1, total_boxes),
        "total_exp": total_exp,
        "current_idx": current_idx,
        "current_code": TIERS[current_idx][0],
        "rank_label": rank_label,
        "class_title": class_title,
        "max_diff": max_diff,
        "cleared_count": len(cleared_indices),
        "cur_tp": cur_tp,
        "next_code": next_code,
        "next_title": next_title,
    }


def build_rpg_card_block(player_name: str, stats: dict) -> str:
    pct = int(round((stats["total_checked"] / stats["total_boxes"]) * 100))
    filled_bars = int(round((stats["cleared_count"] / len(TIERS)) * 28))
    # Pure ASCII progress bars guarantee 100% monospace alignment in VS Code & GitHub
    bar_str = "#" * filled_bars + "-" * (28 - filled_bars)
    tp_bar = "#" * stats["cur_tp"] + "-" * (3 - stats["cur_tp"])

    badge_rank = quote(stats["rank_label"])
    badge_exp = quote(f"{stats['total_exp']:,} EXP ({stats['total_checked']}/{stats['total_boxes']} Quests)")

    s1 = "x" if stats["total_checked"] >= 1 else " "
    s2 = "x" if stats["current_idx"] >= 5 else " "
    s3 = "x" if stats["current_idx"] >= 8 else " "
    s4 = "x" if stats["current_idx"] >= 11 else " "
    s5 = "x" if stats["current_idx"] >= 14 else " "
    s6 = "x" if stats["current_idx"] >= 16 else " "
    s7 = "x" if stats["current_idx"] >= 18 else " "
    s8 = "x" if stats["current_idx"] >= 25 else " "

    inner_w = 88

    def box_row(text: str) -> str:
        padded = f"  {text}"
        return f"║{padded:<{inner_w}.{inner_w}}║"

    top_border = "╔" + ("═" * inner_w) + "╗"
    mid_border = "╠" + ("═" * inner_w) + "╣"
    bot_border = "╚" + ("═" * inner_w) + "╝"

    exp_str = f"{stats['total_exp']:,} ({pct}% Quests Done)"
    rows = [
        top_border,
        box_row(f"ADVENTURER   : {player_name:<26} CLASS     : {stats['class_title']}"),
        box_row(f"CURRENT RANK : {stats['rank_label']:<26} TOTAL EXP : {exp_str}"),
        box_row(f"TIER CLEAR   : [{bar_str}] {stats['cleared_count']:>2} / {len(TIERS)} Tiers Cleared"),
        box_row(f"RANK-UP TRIAL: [{tp_bar}] {stats['cur_tp']}/3 TP in Tier {stats['current_code']:<4} (3 Same-Tier OR 1 Higher-Tier = Rank Up!)"),
        mid_border,
        box_row("PASSIVE SKILL TREE UNLOCKED:"),
        box_row(f" [{s1}] Stage 1: Memory Scanning & Bitmasks    [{s2}] Stage 2: Pointer Chains & AutoAsm"),
        box_row(f" [{s3}] Stage 3: Shared Code Entity Filters    [{s4}] Stage 4: .NET / Unity Mono Dissect"),
        box_row(f" [{s5}] Stage 5: C++ VMT & Internal DLL Hooks  [{s6}] Stage 6: UE4/5 SDK & 3D W2S Math"),
        box_row(f" [{s7}] Stage 7: IL2CPP & Custom Bytecode VMs  [{s8}] Stage 8: Symbolic Z3, O-LLVM & VMI"),
        bot_border,
    ]
    ascii_box = "\n".join(rows)

    return f"""<!-- RPG_CARD_START -->
![Focus](https://img.shields.io/static/v1?label=Focus&message=Offline%20RE%20%26%20Binary%20Auditing&color=blue&style=for-the-badge)
![Current Rank](https://img.shields.io/static/v1?label=Player%20Rank&message={badge_rank}&color=success&style=for-the-badge)
![Total EXP](https://img.shields.io/static/v1?label=Progress&message={badge_exp}&color=orange&style=for-the-badge)
![Web RPG](https://img.shields.io/static/v1?label=Live%20Web%20RPG&message=deavers.github.io%2Fre--dojo&color=blueviolet&style=for-the-badge)
![License](https://img.shields.io/static/v1?label=License&message=MIT&color=green&style=for-the-badge)

```text
{ascii_box}
```
<!-- RPG_CARD_END -->"""


def extract_current_player_name(md_text: str) -> str:
    m = re.search(r"ADVENTURER\s*:\s*([^\s].*?)\s+CLASS\s*:", md_text)
    if m:
        return m.group(1).strip()
    return "New Challenger"


def interactive_add_target():
    print("====================================================================")
    print("  5-AXIS ANTI-INFLATION RUBRIC CALCULATOR (Add Custom Target)")
    print("====================================================================")
    title = input("1. Target Name (Game or Software): ").strip()
    category = input("2. Category (Game / Software / Crackme): ").strip() or "Game"
    engine = input("3. Engine & Architecture (e.g. Unity x64 / Native C++): ").strip() or "Native x64"
    quest = input("4. Quest Objective: ").strip()

    print("\n--- Score the 5 Technical Axes ---")
    arch = float(input("Axis 1 - Arch & Symbols (0.2=Source, 0.5=Symbols, 0.9=Mono, 1.4=Stripped C++, 1.7=IL2CPP, 2.0=Emulator): ") or 0.5)
    mem = float(input("Axis 2 - Memory Indirection (0.1=Static, 0.4=Pointers, 0.8=Lists, 1.2=VMT/GWorld, 1.7=DOD/ECS, 2.0=XOR RAM): ") or 0.4)
    eng = float(input("Axis 3 - Code Complexity (0.1=Scalar, 0.5=2D, 0.8=SharedCode, 1.2=3D W2S, 1.6=DLL Hook, 2.0=LLVM Lifter): ") or 0.5)
    prot = float(input("Axis 4 - Protection (0.0=None, 0.5=AntiDebug, 1.2=Packer/CRC, 1.8=CustomVM/O-LLVM, 2.5=VMProtect): ") or 0.0)
    priv = float(input("Axis 5 - Boundary (0.0=Ring-3, 0.7=PacketCrypto, 1.5=Ring-0/Hypervisor/Silicon): ") or 0.0)

    rubric = {
        "arch_symbols": arch,
        "memory_indirection": mem,
        "engine_complexity": eng,
        "binary_protection": prot,
        "privilege_boundary": priv,
    }
    score, tier = compute_rubric_score(rubric)
    print(f"\n[+] Objective Rubric Calculation: Difficulty = {score:.1f} / 10.0  =>  Assigned Tier: [{tier}]")

    data = json.loads(CUSTOM_DB_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    data.setdefault("targets", []).append(
        {
            "id": f"custom_{len(data['targets']) + 1}",
            "title": title,
            "category": category,
            "engine": engine,
            "rubric": rubric,
            "quest": quest,
            "completed": False,
        }
    )
    CUSTOM_DB_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print("[+] Saved to data/custom_targets.json!")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="RPG Progress Card & Target Sync Engine")
    parser.add_argument("--name", type=str, help="Set Adventurer Name on the RPG Card")
    parser.add_argument("--reset", action="store_true", help="Reset all checkboxes to clean default [ ]")
    parser.add_argument("--preset", type=str, choices=["deavers"], help="Apply preset profile")
    parser.add_argument("--add-target", action="store_true", help="Launch 5-Axis Rubric Target Adder")
    parser.add_argument("--analyze", type=str, help="Auto-grade a .CT table, .cpp/.cs trainer, or .py script")
    parser.add_argument("--from-json", action="store_true", help="Sync checked quests from data/player_progress.json")
    parser.add_argument("--verify-db", action="store_true", help="Verify 5-Axis Rubric scores and Top 200 JSON integrity")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.verify_db:
        if CUSTOM_DB_PATH.exists():
            db = json.loads(CUSTOM_DB_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            targets = db.get("custom_targets", [])
            for t in targets:
                ax = t.get("axes", {})
                calc_score, calc_tier = compute_rubric_score(
                    ax.get("arch", 1.0), ax.get("sym", 1.0), ax.get("ptr", 1.0), ax.get("obf", 1.0), ax.get("ac", 1.0)
                )
                print(f"[OK] Verified Custom Target: {t['name']} -> {calc_score}/10 (Tier {calc_tier})")
        if TOP100_DB_PATH.exists():
            games = json.loads(TOP100_DB_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            assert len(games) == 200, f"Expected 200 games, got {len(games)}"
            print(f"[OK] Verified Top 200 Popular Games Database: {len(games)} entries valid.")
        return

    if args.analyze:
        from auto_artifact_grader import grade_file, print_report
        target_p = Path(args.analyze)
        if not target_p.exists():
            print(f"[!] File not found: {target_p}")
            return
        if target_p.is_dir():
            for child in sorted(target_p.rglob("*")):
                if child.is_file() and child.suffix.lower() in {".ct", ".cea", ".cpp", ".hpp", ".c", ".cs", ".py"}:
                    print_report(grade_file(child))
        else:
            print_report(grade_file(target_p))
        return

    if args.add_target:
        interactive_add_target()

    if not README_PATH.exists():
        print("[!] README.md not found!")
        return

    md_text = README_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    player_name = args.name or extract_current_player_name(md_text)

    if args.reset:
        player_name = args.name or "New Challenger"
        # Reset all quest checkboxes outside the RPG card
        md_text = re.sub(r"^(\s*-\s*)\[[xX]\]", r"\1[ ]", md_text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    elif args.preset == "deavers":
        player_name = "deavers"
        md_text = re.sub(r"^(\s*-\s*)\[[xX]\]", r"\1[ ]", md_text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
        lines = md_text.splitlines()
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            if re.match(r"^\s*-\s*\[\s\]", line):
                if any(kw in line for kw in DEAVERS_PRESET_KEYWORDS):
                    lines[i] = re.sub(r"^(\s*-\s*)\[\s\]", r"\1[x]", line, count=1)
        md_text = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    elif args.from_json and PROGRESS_JSON_PATH.exists():
        saved = json.loads(PROGRESS_JSON_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        player_name = args.name or saved.get("player_name", player_name)
        keywords = saved.get("completed_keywords", [])
        if keywords:
            md_text = re.sub(r"^(\s*-\s*)\[[xX]\]", r"\1[ ]", md_text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
            lines = md_text.splitlines()
            for i, line in enumerate(lines):
                if re.match(r"^\s*-\s*\[\s\]", line):
                    if any(kw in line for kw in keywords):
                        lines[i] = re.sub(r"^(\s*-\s*)\[\s\]", r"\1[x]", line, count=1)
            md_text = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

    # Sync Top 200 Popular Games table
    top100_block = render_top100_section()
    if top100_block:
        if "<!-- TOP100_GAMES_START -->" in md_text:
            md_text = re.sub(
                r"<!-- TOP100_GAMES_START -->.*?<!-- TOP100_GAMES_END -->",
                top100_block,
                md_text,
                flags=re.DOTALL,
            )
        else:
            anchor = "## 💻 Software, File Formats & Binary Auditing Arsenal (Programs & Utilities)"
            if anchor in md_text:
                md_text = md_text.replace(anchor, top100_block + "\n\n---\n\n" + anchor)

    # Sync custom targets table
    custom_block = render_custom_targets_section()
    if custom_block and "<!-- CUSTOM_TARGETS_START -->" in md_text:
        md_text = re.sub(
            r"<!-- CUSTOM_TARGETS_START -->.*?<!-- CUSTOM_TARGETS_END -->",
            custom_block,
            md_text,
            flags=re.DOTALL,
        )

    tier_counts = parse_tier_checkboxes(md_text)
    stats = calculate_progression(tier_counts)
    new_card = build_rpg_card_block(player_name, stats)

    if "<!-- RPG_CARD_START -->" in md_text and "<!-- RPG_CARD_END -->" in md_text:
        md_text = re.sub(
            r"<!-- RPG_CARD_START -->.*?<!-- RPG_CARD_END -->",
            new_card,
            md_text,
            flags=re.DOTALL,
        )

    README_PATH.write_text(md_text, encoding="utf-8")

    # Save persistent progress snapshot to data/player_progress.json
    checked_lines = [
        line.strip() for line in md_text.splitlines() if re.match(r"^\s*-\s*\[[xX]\]", line)
    ]
    PROGRESS_JSON_PATH.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "player_name": player_name,
                "rank_label": stats["rank_label"],
                "current_tier": stats["current_code"],
                "total_exp": stats["total_exp"],
                "quests_completed": stats["total_checked"],
                "quests_total": stats["total_boxes"],
                "tiers_cleared": stats["cleared_count"],
                "current_tier_tp": stats["cur_tp"],
                "completed_quest_lines": checked_lines,
                "tier_breakdown": tier_counts,
            },
            indent=2,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print("====================================================================")
    print(f"  [+] RPG Card Synchronized in README.md & data/player_progress.json!")
    print(f"      Adventurer : {player_name}")
    print(f"      Rank       : {stats['rank_label']}")
    print(f"      Total EXP  : {stats['total_exp']:,} EXP ({stats['total_checked']}/{stats['total_boxes']} Quests)")
    print(f"      Tiers Done : {stats['cleared_count']} / {len(TIERS)} (Current Tier TP: {stats['cur_tp']}/3)")
    print("====================================================================")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`build_all.bat`:

```bat
@echo off
setlocal enabledelayedexpansion

echo ============================================================================
echo   RE:DOJO - Hardened OPSEC Build Script (MSVC / MinGW)
echo ============================================================================

if not exist "build\bin" mkdir "build\bin"
if not exist "build\obj" mkdir "build\obj"

:: Try to locate MSVC vcvars64.bat via vswhere
set "VSWHERE=%ProgramFiles(x86)%\Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe"
if exist "%VSWHERE%" (
    for /f "usebackq tokens=*" %%i in (`"%VSWHERE%" -latest -property installationPath`) do (
        set "VSINSTALL=%%i"
    )
)

where cl.exe >nul 2>nul
if %ERRORLEVEL% NEQ 0 (
    if defined VSINSTALL (
        if exist "!VSINSTALL!\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat" (
            echo [*] Initializing Visual Studio x64 Developer Environment...
            call "!VSINSTALL!\VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat" >nul
        )
    )
)

where cl.exe >nul 2>nul
if %ERRORLEVEL% EQU 0 (
    echo [+] Compiling with MSVC cl.exe ^(x64, OPSEC Path-Stripped ^& Deterministic PE^)...
    set "CFLAGS=/nologo /EHsc /std:c++20 /O1 /Brepro /Fo:build\obj\"
    set "LFLAGS=/link /INCREMENTAL:NO /Brepro /PDBALTPATH:%%_PDB%%"

    cl !CFLAGS! "Course_Solutions\Lab01_Static_Scalars\lab01_static_scalars.cpp" /Fe:build\bin\lab01_static_scalars.exe !LFLAGS!
    cl !CFLAGS! "Course_Solutions\Lab02_Data_Types_and_Bitmasks\lab02_datatypes_bitmasks.cpp" /Fe:build\bin\lab02_datatypes_bitmasks.exe !LFLAGS!

    cl !CFLAGS! "Crackmes_and_CTF\02_Mathematical_Lock\crackme02_serial_math.cpp" /Fe:build\bin\crackme02_serial_math.exe !LFLAGS!

    cl !CFLAGS! "Crackmes_and_CTF\05_AntiDebug_Sentinel\crackme05_antidebug.cpp" /Fe:build\bin\crackme05_antidebug.exe !LFLAGS!
    cl !CFLAGS! "Crackmes_and_CTF\06_Custom_Stack_VM\crackme06_bytecode_vm.cpp" /Fe:build\bin\crackme06_bytecode_vm.exe !LFLAGS!

    echo.

where g++.exe >nul 2>nul
if %ERRORLEVEL% EQU 0 (
    echo [+] Compiling with MinGW g++ ^(OPSEC Path-Stripped^)...
    set "GFLAGS=-std=c++20 -O1 -ffile-prefix-map=.= -fdebug-prefix-map=.="
    g++ !GFLAGS! "Course_Solutions/Lab01_Static_Scalars/lab01_static_scalars.cpp" -o "build/bin/lab01_static_scalars.exe"

    g++ !GFLAGS! "Course_Solutions/Lab07_ReClass_and_VMT_Hooking/lab07_vtable_reclass.cpp" -o "build/bin/lab07_vtable_reclass.exe"
    g++ !GFLAGS! "Course_Solutions/Lab08_3D_Math_and_WorldToScreen/lab08_3d_w2s_simulator.cpp" -o "build/bin/lab08_3d_w2s_simulator.exe"
    g++ !GFLAGS! "Course_Solutions/Lab09_Internal_DLL_and_Hooks/lab09_internal_hook_target.cpp" -o "build/bin/lab09_internal_hook_target.exe"
    g++ !GFLAGS! "Course_Solutions/Lab10_Integrity_and_AntiTamper_Boss/lab10_integrity_boss.cpp" -o "build/bin/lab10_integrity_boss.exe"

    goto :eof
)

echo [!] Neither MSVC (cl.exe) nor MinGW (g++.exe) was found in PATH.
